### [1] [Aperiodicity is sufficient for macroscopic thermalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13462)
*Amit Vikram*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种宏观可观测量（如粗粒化电荷密度）在有限时间内热化的普遍机制，该机制基于初始态量子动力学的两个基本要素：（1）非周期性（提供可计算的度量）和（2）希尔伯特空间的动力学部分遍历探索。这一机制仅需关于返回概率在有限时间范围内较小的可观测量无关信息，即可预测几乎所有初始系综中的态和几乎所有有限时间区间内宏观可观测量的平衡，并将宏观热化纳入可操作量子统计力学的范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子统计力学对热化的描述通常依赖于无限长时间极限下的本征态热化假说（ETH）或有效维度等概念，这些标准在有限观测时间内无法直接验证或预测。作者希望将宏观热化的研究纳入"可操作量子统计力学"的框架——该框架此前已被发展用于提供微观热化的有限可计算判据，从而在有限的、可观测的时间尺度上给出热化的可计算预测。

Method: 作者识别出宏观可观测量有限时间热化的两个关键动力学要素：(1) 非周期性（aperiodicity），作为可计算的度量；(2) 动力学部分遍历探索（dynamical partially ergodic exploration）希尔伯特空间。核心方法是利用初始态系综的返回概率在有限时间范围内很小的信息（可观测量无关），建立均衡化的充分条件。文中同时展示了该框架如何推广标准结果：当采用更强的有效维度（初始态在能量本征基中的离域程度）条件时，可恢复无限长时间热化的经典结论。

Result: 该方法预测了几乎所有初始系综中的态和几乎所有有限且较长时间区间内的宏观可观测量达到均衡。作为一个特例，它恢复了基于较强有效维度条件的无限长时间热化标准结果。作者还提供了该方法的整体特征化：将(1)记忆丧失探针的衰减（理论上或实验上可计算的度量）、(2)希尔伯特空间中可观测量或态对齐的基本不变机制，以及(3)不同自然形式（经典和量子）热化的预测三者联系起来，大多数情况在热力学极限下严格恢复传统的基于本征态描述的无限时间热化，但在有限观测时间内提供更强的可获取预测。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：宏观热化不再仅属于抽象理论范畴，而是可以被纳入可操作量子统计力学的框架中。该方法提供了一种新的、有限时间可验证的热化机制描述，将微观动力学性质（非周期性、部分遍历探索）与宏观热化现象直接关联，并在热力学极限下与传统的本征态描述兼容，同时提供了传统方法无法给出的有限时间预测能力。

Abstract: We identify a general mechanism for the finite-time thermalization of macroscopic observables, such as coarse-grained charge densities, in terms of elementary forms of the quantum dynamics of initial states: (1) aperiodicity, which provides a computable measure of (2) a dynamical partially ergodic exploration of the Hilbert space. Specifically, this mechanism predicts the equilibration of all (concentrated) macroscopic observables, in almost all states in an initial ensemble and almost all times within finite and longer intervals, given only the observable-independent information that the return probability of the ensemble of initial states is small over a finite time range. As a special case, it also accesses standard results on equilibration over infinitely long times in terms of (stronger versions of) the effective dimension of initial state delocalization in the energy eigenbasis. Our results incorporate macroscopic thermalization into the domain of operational quantum statistical mechanics, recently developed to provide finitely computable criteria for microscopic thermalization. We discuss an overall characterization of this approach as establishing connections between (1) the decay of a (theoretically or experimentally) computable probe indicating memorylessness, (2) a fundamental invariant mechanism in terms of the alignment of observables or states in the Hilbert space, and (3) predicting different natural forms of (classical and) quantum thermalization, most of which rigorously recover conventional eigenstate-based descriptions of infinite-time thermalization as a special case but provide stronger accessible predictions over finite observation times in the thermodynamic limit.

</details>


### [2] [Embedding Stabilizer Codes and Leakage Correction in Multilevel Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12450)
*Ali Abu-Nada,Lian-Ao Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了任意[[n,k,d]]稳定子码可以等距嵌入到单个D维系统中，同时保留其完整的纠错结构，并推导出精确泄漏校正的充要条件，建立了多能级量子系统中量子纠错的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泄漏（leakage）超出计算子空间是多能级量子硬件中的主要误差来源，现有理论缺乏对泄漏可校正性的统一理解。

Method: 通过证明任意稳定子码可等距嵌入单个D维系统，并推导泄漏可校正的充要条件（泄漏不区分逻辑态时即可精确校正）。

Result: 证明了稳定子码的等距嵌入保结构性质，并给出了精确泄漏校正的充要条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为多能级量子系统中的量子纠错建立了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: Leakage beyond the computational subspace is a major source of error in multilevel quantum hardware. We show that any \( [[n,k,d]] \) stabilizer code can be embedded isometrically into a single \(D\)-dimensional system while preserving its complete error-correcting structure. We further derive a necessary and sufficient condition for exact leakage correction, proving that leakage is correctable precisely when it does not distinguish between logical states. These results establish a unified framework for quantum error correction in multilevel quantum systems.

</details>


### [3] [Biorthogonal-only Floquet Dynamical Quantum Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07882)
*Jiangrong Wen,Qidong Yuan,Zi-Xiang Hu,Jian-Jun Dong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过精确可解的周期驱动非厄米Su-Schrieffer-Heeger链模型，证明了双正交与自归一动力学量子相变（DQPT）并非必然伴随发生，发现了仅存在双正交Floquet DQPT的有限参数区域，确立了二者之间的根本区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米动力学量子相变对内积选择有内在敏感性，但以往研究认为双正交和自归一的DQPT总是同时出现。作者旨在挑战这一共识，证明这两种动力学临界性并非始终一致，从而揭示出新的非平衡量子现象。

Method: 采用一个精确可解的周期性驱动非厄米Su-Schrieffer-Heeger链，分别计算双正交和自归一Loschmidt回波及其速率。通过解析求解临界条件，将双正交Floquet DQPT的临界条件锁定到有效Floquet哈密顿量的异常线（exceptional lines）上，并对比两种DQPT在每个驱动周期内的临界时间数量。

Result: 发现了一个有限的"双正交专属"Floquet DQPT区域，在此区域中双正交Loschmidt速率出现非解析行为而自归一Loschmidt速率保持光滑。双正交Floquet DQPT的临界条件与有效Floquet哈密顿量的异常线相关联，而自归一临界性没有对应的谱边界。在每个临界动量下，双正交DQPT每个驱动周期内有一对临界时间，而自归一DQPT仅有单个临界时间。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了双正交与自归一DQPT之间的根本区别，推翻了先前认为二者总是在相同参数区域同时出现的认识，为非厄米系统中新的非平衡量子现象开辟了研究途径。

Abstract: Non-Hermitian dynamical quantum phase transitions (DQPTs) are intrinsically sensitive to the choice of inner product under nonunitary time evolution. Although the biorthogonal formulation based on associated states provides a normalized Loschmidt echo with a probabilistic interpretation, previous studies have found biorthogonal and self-normal DQPTs to occur in the same parameter regimes, suggesting that the two forms of dynamical criticality are concomitant. Here we demonstrate that this is not the case. In an exactly solvable periodically driven non-Hermitian Su-Schrieffer-Heeger chain, we uncover a finite biorthogonal-only Floquet DQPT regime, where the biorthogonal Loschmidt rate becomes nonanalytic while the self-normal Loschmidt rate remains smooth. The critical conditions are obtained analytically, showing that the onset of biorthogonal Floquet DQPTs is locked to the exceptional lines of the effective Floquet Hamiltonian, whereas self-normal criticality has no corresponding spectral boundary. Moreover, for each critical momentum, the biorthogonal DQPT exhibits a pair of critical times within every driving period, whereas the self-normal DQPT exhibits only one. Our results establish a fundamental distinction between biorthogonal and self-normal DQPTs, thereby opening a route toward new nonequilibrium quantum phenomena in non-Hermitian systems.

</details>


### [4] [Spatially Dense, Continuous-Variable Quantum Computing with Solid State Spin Nonlinearities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12504)
*Hamza Raniwala,Ethan G Arnault,Dirk R. Englund,Matthew E. Trusheim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于应变敏感固态自旋与纳米机械谐振腔耦合的玻色子量子信息处理架构，利用自旋作为非线性元素产生非经典机械态（猫态），实现芯片上的最近邻连接、通用量子计算控制和读出，并通过玻色子纠错方案与小型化谐振器实现高密度逻辑量子比特，最终给出可扩展量子信息处理的性能指标路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米机械结构具有高品质因子，是玻色子编码量子信息的理想介质，但机械模式本质上缺乏所需的非线性相互作用，难以扩展到多量子比特体系，阻碍了实用量子计算的实现。因此需要引入非线性元素来克服这一根本限制。

Method: 利用应变敏感的固态自旋作为非线性耦合元素，与纳米机械谐振器集成在芯片上。通过自旋与机械模式的应变耦合产生非经典机械猫态，构建最近邻连接的猫态量子比特架构。同时设计了通用量子计算所需的控制与读出体系，并利用玻色子纠错方案提高逻辑量子比特的空间密度。

Result: 该架构能够实现芯片上机械猫态量子比特之间的最近邻连通性以及通用量子计算所需的控制和读出。通过结合玻色子纠错的高效率与纳米机械谐振器和自旋量子比特的小型化优势，可实现高空间密度的逻辑量子比特。此外，论文确定了实现高量子比特密度下容错纠错阈值所需的性能指标。

Conclusion: 这项工作为可扩展量子信息处理提供了一条清晰路径：通过自旋-机械混合架构弥补机械模式非线性的缺失，实现基于玻色子编码的规模化量子计算，并指明了迈向实用化所需的关键性能参数。

Abstract: Nanomechanical structures have been investigated as a method of achieving long-lived quantum excitations at radio frequencies. Their high quality factors are especially intriguing as a medium for bosonic encoding of quantum information. However, to leading order, mechanical modes typically lack the nonlinearities necessary to achieve interaction between bosonic channels and thus are limited in their ability to scale to the many-qubit regime necessary for practical quantum computing. In this work, we propose and describe an approach for bosonic quantum information processing that uses strain-sensitive solid-state spins as nonlinear elements to produce the relevant nonclassical mechanical states. We outline the architecture required to achieve nearest-neighbor connectivity between mechanical cat-state qubits on-chip, as well as the control and readout architecture required for universal quantum computation. In addition, we show that this architecture can allow for a high spatial density of logical qubits by leveraging both the efficiency of bosonic error correction schemes and the small sizes of the constituent nanomechanical resonators and spin qubits. Finally, we identify the necessary performance metrics that will enable error-correction thresholds at high qubit densities, illuminating a path towards scalable quantum information processing.

</details>


### [5] [Pulse Engineering of Quantum Many-Body Dynamics: Emergent Scar States, Entanglement, and Nonstabilizerness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12559)
*Prasant Mallik,Arkaprava Sil,Sudipto Singha Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过脉冲工程技术构造有效哈密顿量，使其在混沌的Heisenberg(XXX)链（含局域杂质）与ZX哈密顿量之间连续插值，在此过程中发现了多族具有不同纠缠和nonstabilizerness特性的可解析非典型本征态，并揭示了量子混沌与量子资源产生之间的部分解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管过去几十年已引入多种多体哈密顿量来研究量子混沌、非典型本征态和量子资源，但在单一微观模型中系统地工程化并连续调谐这些性质仍鲜有探索。作者旨在通过脉冲工程技术弥补这一空白。

Method: 采用脉冲工程方案构造有效哈密顿量，在混沌XXX链（含局域杂质）与ZX哈密顿量之间连续插值。沿此插值路径，识别多族解析可处理的非典型本征态，并分析其纠缠熵与稳定子Rényi熵（nonstabilizerness）特性。

Result: 在XXX极限下，这些态在纠缠和稳定子Rényi熵上展现精确平台，对应于长程价键固态(VBS)态的相干叠加；随脉冲强度增加，低能谱出现新的一组近似纠缠平台；在ZX极限下发现一对长程纠缠的稳定子本征态（彩虹疤痕态）。脉冲工程保留原始模型的混沌与非混沌区域，但两种区域在动力学纠缠与非稳定子产生上几乎相同，表明量子混沌与量子资源产生部分解耦。

Conclusion: 脉冲工程可作为生成具有结构化本征态和可调谐量子资源的多体哈密顿量的通用框架，为量子资源工程和非平衡量子物理研究提供了新途径。

Abstract: Understanding and coherently controlling the properties of interacting quantum many-body systems is a central challenge in non-equilibrium quantum physics. While in the past decades, a wide range of many-body Hamiltonians have been introduced to study quantum chaos, atypical eigenstates, and quantum resources, systematically engineering and continuously tuning these properties within a single microscopic model remains largely unexplored. Here, we employ a pulse engineering scheme to construct an effective Hamiltonian that continuously interpolates between a chaotic Heisenberg (XXX) chain with a local impurity and the ZX Hamiltonian. Along this interpolation, we identify several families of analytically tractable atypical eigenstates embedded in the excited-state spectrum with distinct entanglement and nonstabilizerness properties. In the XXX limit, these states exhibit exact plateaus in both entanglement and stabilizer Rényi entropy and correspond to coherent superpositions of long-range valence-bond solid (VBS) states. As the pulse strength increases, the effective Hamiltonians exhibit a hierarchy of new set of approximate entanglement plateaus in the low-energy spectrum. Interestingly, in the fully pulse-engineered ZX limit, we uncover a distinct pair of long-range entangled stabilizer eigenstates, corresponding to rainbow scar states. We further show that pulse engineering preserves the distinct chaotic and non-chaotic regimes of the original model, while that is largely absent in the dynamical generation of entanglement and nonstabilizerness. The pulse-engineered models generate nearly identical quantum resources in both regimes, revealing a partial decoupling between quantum chaos and quantum-resource generation. Our results establish pulse engineering as a versatile framework for generating many-body Hamiltonians with structured eigenstates and tunable quantum resources.

</details>


### [6] [Quantum Tanner Codes at Moderate Blocklength](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12509)
*Feroz Ahmed Mian,Vaishnavi L. Addala,Arman Meraj,Adhiraj Chadha,Stefan Krastanov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过两种互补方法（左-右 Cayley 复形描述与"提升"视角）显式构造了具有良好码率和距离的量子 Tanner (QT) 码，在中等等块长度区间找到多个新码实例（如 [[480,8,(≤21,≤21)]] 等），并提供了开源库 QuantumExpanders.jl。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错码是容错量子计算的关键组成部分。已有的 QT 码构造方法有限，且在中等等块长度范围内的性能表现尚未充分探索。作者希望通过两种互补途径系统构造具有良好参数的新 QT 码实例，并评估其在实际噪声模型下的解码性能。

Method: 采用两种互补的构造方法：(1) 左-右 Cayley 复形 (LRCC) 描述；(2) "提升"视角——将种子 Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) 码通过有限群 G 的交换左-右正则作用提升。利用 GAP 的 SmallGrp 库对非阿贝尔群进行广泛搜索，覆盖中等块长度 n∈[500,1000]。使用 sQetch（随机化距离估计器）进行高达 3.5 亿次试验验证距离上界，并使用 Tesseract 解码器评估伪阈值。

Result: 发现了多个新 QT 码实例，包括 [[480,8,(≤21,≤21)]]、[[504,4,(≤36,≤27)]]、[[672,4,(≤48,≤28)]]、[[720,6,(≤30,≤30)]] 和 [[864,8,(≤39,≤31)]]，校验权重范围为 9 到 20。在唯象噪声下伪阈值为 3.6%-4.6%，电路级噪声下为 0.14%-0.27%，与更短码长的先前结果相当。

Conclusion: QT 码在中等等块长度下能够实现良好的码率-距离权衡，其伪阈值与更短码长结果相当，表明该码族在中等规模量子纠错中具有竞争力。作者同时发布了 QuantumExpanders.jl 开源库以促进后续研究和复现。

Abstract: We present explicit constructions of quantum Tanner (QT) codes with good rate and distance, obtained through two complementary approaches: the left-right Cayley complex (LRCC) description and the "lifting" perspective, in which a seed Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) code is lifted by commuting left-right regular actions of a finite group $\mathcal{G}$. Through an extensive search over non-abelian groups from GAP's SmallGrp library, we investigate the moderate-blocklength regime ($n \in [500,1000]$) and identify several new code instances with distance upper bounds exceeding $20$. These include $[[480,8,(\leq 21,\leq 21)]]$, $[[504,4,(\leq 36,\leq 27)]]$, $[[672,4,(\leq 48,\leq 28)]]$, $[[720,6,(\leq 30,\leq 30)]]$, and $[[864,8,(\leq 39,\leq 31)]]$, with these bounds obtained using up to $350$ million trials of sQetch, a randomized distance estimator. The code instances presented have check weights ranging from $9$ to $20$. Using the Tesseract decoder, we estimate pseudo-thresholds of $3.6\%$-$4.6\%$ under phenomenological noise and $0.14\%$-$0.27\%$ under circuit-level noise, comparable to prior results at shorter code lengths. We also provide QuantumExpanders.jl, an open-source Julia library for constructing QT codes and explicit constructions of Ramanujan graphs.

</details>


### [7] [Flux control of measurement back-action and Leggett-Garg correlations in chiral quantum walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12519)
*Paolo Luppi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究手性连续时间量子行走中规范不变通量对单顶点顺序测量的干涉控制作用，建立了双时统计量（含测量反作用与Leggett-Garg关联子）与返回振幅、局部谱测度及规范不变闭合行走干涉之间的精确联系，并发现了通量工程化可饱和Lüders界(3/2)的普适机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解规范不变通量（Peierls相位）如何影响量子行走中的干涉以及局部测量反作用和时间量子关联，并探索通量作为工程工具来操控量子测量统计量的可能性。

Method: 采用二分观测量区分返回原顶点与占据补空间，对初始局域化的行走者求解完整的双时统计量；利用返回振幅联系局部谱测度与闭合行走干涉；构建Krylov子空间约化方法分析Lüders界饱和条件；对通量循环采用绕组数展开分析通量对比的奇偶依赖行为。

Result: 短时间主干扰与Peierls相位无关，通量敏感性通过闭合行走干涉在更高阶出现。识别出图无关的Lüders界饱和机制：通量可将根动态约化为具有等谱权重的二维平衡Krylov子空间，在双通量菱形图上精确实现。通量循环中偶数环的通量对比在t^N阶开始，奇数环在t^{2N}阶开始；半通量可将四站点循环驱动至Lüders界。

Conclusion: 规范不变通量可作为工程局部测量反作用和时间量子关联的有效资源，为量子信息处理中的测量调控提供理论依据。

Abstract: Gauge-invariant fluxes control interference in chiral continuous-time quantum walks. We investigate how they affect sequential measurements at a single vertex, using a dichotomic observable that distinguishes return to that vertex from occupation of its complement. For a walker initially localized at the measured vertex, the complete two-time statistics, including measurement back-action and Leggett--Garg correlators, are determined exactly by the return amplitude, connecting temporal correlations to the local spectral measure and gauge-invariant closed-walk interference. At short times, the leading disturbance is independent of the Peierls phases, whereas flux sensitivity enters at higher orders through interference among closed walks. We further identify a graph-independent sufficient mechanism for saturating the Lüders bound: flux can reduce the rooted dynamics to a balanced two-dimensional Krylov subspace with equal spectral weights, yielding a constructive flux-engineering criterion for attaining the Lüders bound of $3/2$ at finite times. The mechanism is realized exactly on a two-flux diamond graph, where destructive interference renders additional rooted modes dark and the local back-action depends on relative combinations of the two independent fluxes. For flux-threaded cycles, an exact winding-number expansion reveals a parity-dependent onset: the leading flux contrast occurs generically at order $t^N$ for even cycles and $t^{2N}$ for odd cycles. Across the cycles examined, flux can either enhance the maximal Leggett--Garg violation or shift strong violations to earlier measurement times, with half flux driving the four-site cycle to the Lüders bound. These results establish gauge-invariant flux as a resource for engineering local measurement back-action and temporal quantum correlations.

In composable finite-key analyses governed by the Generalized Entropy Accumulation Theorem (GEAT), traditional adaptive post-selection heuristics either violate strict independence conditions or incur massive second-order penalties that collapse the secret key rate. To overcome this, we introduce a reference-only topological post-selection oracle. By modeling the constellation as a Cellular Sheaf and applying Topological Data Analysis (TDA), our protocol derives a public acceptance event ($Ω$) exclusively from classical beacon telemetry. To rigorously validate this mechanism, we develop a modular simulation framework equipped with stochastic noise injection and an explicit GEAT security ledger. Simulations across 2,000-5,000 km ISL separations compare the same Hodge-Koopman gate with TDA disabled and enabled. At 2,000 km, the median conditional candidate rates are $2.14 \times 10^{-6}$ and $5.87 \times 10^{-7}$ bit per emitted pulse, respectively; both configurations return zero at 3,000-5,000 km. TDA is active in all 4,788 evaluated windows, but does not extend the positive-candidate range in this scenario. These exported rates are conditional numerical candidates: the full protocol-level composable-security proof remains incomplete and the certified composable rate is therefore zero throughout.

Abstract: Growing integration of distributed energy resources increases power-system variability and uncertainty. During disturbances, these effects can intensify generation-load imbalances and cascading failures. Controlled islanding limits their propagation by partitioning a compromised grid into connected, electrically sustainable islands. However, classical methods face rapidly growing computational costs as network size and island count increase. Quantum optimization offers an alternative for exploring this combinatorial partition space. Yet monolithic quantum formulations encode all assignment decisions in one circuit, causing qubit demand and circuit complexity to scale with network size. In this study, a qubit-bounded sequential distributed quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) framework is proposed to tackle coherent controlled islanding under limited quantum resources. It formulates the optimization as boundary-conditioned regional quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) subproblems that are solved sequentially within a fixed qubit budget. Thus, circuit width remains independent of network size, with aggregate quantum workload scaling linearly on bounded-degree networks. Evaluation covers eleven IEEE systems from 9 to 300 buses using IBM quantum computing resources, with Gurobi and monolithic QAOA as references. Across all systems, the framework recovers feasible Gurobi-optimal partitions under noise, confirming the resilience of its solution quality. The results further show that the proposed method substantially reduces quantum-resource demand and circuit complexity relative to monolithic QAOA, allowing large islanding problems to be addressed within current hardware limits. The proposed framework provides a feasible and scalable pathway for quantum optimization in large-scale power systems.

Abstract: Fixed quantum feature maps are increasingly inserted into diffusion denoisers, but standard image benchmarks do not reveal which structural constraint limits them. The obstruction is a property of the noise-conditioned denoising target rather than static representability: the floor is re-derived at each noise scale because the target's parity content changes with noise. Abstract: The data processing inequality is a fundamental property that describes the loss of information through noisy channels. In the high-$Q$ limit, $\mathcal{C}_{\max}\simeq1-π^{2}/(16Q^{2})$, so the entanglement is robust against cavity loss; in the low-$Q$ limit it decays exponentially to zero, consistent with the irreversible-reservoir character of a continuous field, where maximal entanglement is unattainable. Abstract: Determining whether a target unitary can be implemented within a prescribed quantum circuit architecture is a fundamental problem in quantum information, with direct implications for optimisation and compilation of quantum circuits, and hardware-efficient quantum computation. Here we introduce a general framework to define quantum circuit architecture witnesses, which certify the incompatibility of a unitary transformation with a specified quantum circuit architecture. We formulate the witness construction as a semidefinite program by maximising the fidelity between the Choi state of the target unitary and those of tested circuits. The resulting witnesses provide practical and quantitative certificates of incompatibility, implying lower bounds on implementation resources such as the gate count or circuit depth, and can also be used experimentally to benchmark quantum devices by certifying that an implemented unitary channel goes beyond the capabilities of a given circuit architecture. For Clifford unitaries, we exploit the stabiliser formalism to reduce the construction to linear programming, enabling both more efficient numerical certification for circuits containing on the order of seven two-qubit gates, and analytical witnesses for some families of architectures made of an arbitrary number of gates.

Abstract: Existing quantum algorithms for simulating dynamical systems -- from Hamiltonian simulation to linear and nonlinear differential equations solvers -- simulate Markovian dynamics, in which the system's future evolution depends solely on its current state. We turn our attention to developing quantum algorithms for non-Markovian dynamical systems where the system's future evolution depends on its past history and thus has memory. Specifically, we develop efficient algorithms for linear Volterra integro-differential equations (VIDEs) with a convolution memory kernel that output a quantum state encoding the state description over a time interval or at a particular time. Given efficient circuits for the problem inputs, our algorithms achieve an exponential speedup in system size over existing classical algorithms.
  We develop an algorithm for general kernels assuming that $\textsf{M} < 1$, where $\textsf{M}$ characterizes the strength of the memory term relative to the dissipation of the Markovian part of the dynamics. We complement this with lower bounds for general-kernel VIDEs when $\textsf{M} \geq 1$, showing that the problem becomes intractable for a family of systems. However, by specializing to structured kernels which admit concise decompositions over exponentials, we develop efficient quantum algorithms even when $\textsf M \geq 1$ by converting the VIDE into a larger set of ODEs, a procedure which we call Markovianization. As an application of the overall framework, we discuss the Mori-Zwanzig formalism used in open quantum systems and fluid dynamics. Overall, our results expand the range of dynamical systems that quantum computers can simulate efficiently.

Abstract: To achieve large-scale fault-tolerant quantum computation, it may be easier to combine many small sets of qubits than to construct a single large set. In these regimes, the time or error budget of the overall quantum computation may be dominated by non-local operations. Hence, it is worthwhile to minimize the number of these operations. We consider the case where both non-local and local connectivity may be arbitrarily restricted, and give an asymptotically optimal synthesis method for distributed CNOT and Clifford circuits, based on block-matrix Gaussian elimination. We extend this to all Clifford+RZ circuits by generalizing the Pauli exponential circuit representation; this naturally integrates with existing methods for optimizing T-count. Abstract: In quantum physics the order in which different operations occur can be placed in superposition. The resulting processes have an indefinite causal order and are both of fundamental interest and can be viewed as a novel quantum resource that enables a variety of new protocols. Here we report an experimental implementation of one such protocol, where we perform BB84-like quantum cryptography by placing Alice and Bob's measurement-and-preparation operations in a photonic quantum SWITCH. By embedding Alice and Bob within the quantum SWITCH, the protocol achieves an average eavesdropper detection probability of $0.15 \pm 0.02$ per shared qubit, with eavesdropper detection performed through measurements of the control qubit rather than by comparing the key. Unlike the standard BB84 and related schemes, which detect eavesdropping by publicly revealing and discarding a fraction of the raw key, our approach requires no disclosure of key material: every retained qubit can, in principle, be tested for eavesdropping while remaining available for key generation. The experiment relies on a new measurement technique that allows the polarization of a photon to be measured inside the quantum SWITCH without destroying path coherence. Although the present implementation does not yet constitute a secure quantum key distribution protocol, owing to the post-selection required for measurements within the quantum SWITCH, it provides a proof of principle that indefinite causal order can be exploited to detect eavesdropping without sacrificing key bits.

Abstract: Cone structures on differentiable manifolds are fields of cones in the tangent bundle. A cone structure is isotrivial, modeled on a fixed immersed submanifold of the projective space, if at each point the projectivised cone is projectively equivalent to that submanifold. We consider cone structures arising from ordinary differential equations via a canonical construction. We prove that isotrivial cone structures in this class, modeled on generic curves in the $n$-dimensional projective space or ruled surfaces in the three-dimensional projective space, are flat. We also discuss applications to causal geometries in four dimensions and dispersionless Lax systems.

Abstract: The main result of this article shows that the quiver mutation class of a malleable real Morsification uniquely determines the integral monodromy module of a plane curve singularity. Abstract: We study whether the topology underlying a Calabi-Yau manifold can support natural geometric positivity or negativity structures. Abstract: We establish lower bounds for the Smith--Thom deficiency of the Hilbert square of a maximal nonsingular real projective variety. These bounds recover the known surface case and provide new obstructions to Smith-Thom maximality in every dimension at least two. As applications, we prove that the Hilbert square of any real abelian variety of dimension at least two is not Smith-Thom maximal. We further show that the deficiencies of the Hilbert squares of maximal real abelian varieties grow exponentially with the dimension. We also obtain nonmaximality results for the Hilbert squares of certain Cartesian products.

Abstract: We formulate an elementary collision-generated compression principle for homogeneous Keller maps. For Thompson's 24-variable cubic-homogeneous map, exact polarization gives growth \(2,4,11,20,20\), and the resulting subalgebra is exactly MacFarlane's 20-dimensional invariant subspace. Thus the known \(24\)-to-\(20\) compression is recovered canonically from the collision itself. Although the resulting 40-variable lift had already been noted, we give an explicit 350-monomial homogeneous quartic for which Zhao's Vanishing Conjecture fails, together with an exact gradient collision over \(\Q(i)\). Over \(\Q\), \(\Q(i)\), and \(\C\), no proper invariant linear subspace containing this collision is available before the unchanged symmetric lift. No global minimality is claimed. The principal explicit calculations are checked by an accompanying exact calculation.

### [68] [Rigidity for Lie algebras of locally finite derivations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12567)
*Mohamed Ali Belabbas*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明：域上有限生成交换代数A的导子代数Der(A)中，任何由局部有限元生成的有限生成Lie子代数都是有限维的。进一步，对于有限个局部有限导子生成的Lie子代数，有限维性、局部有限作用和所有元素局部有限三者等价。另一个关键定理是：元素全为局部幂零的Lie子代数必为可解的，且当A约化时导长度不超过dim A，界是紧的。由此推论出仿射簇自同构群中有限个连通代数子群生成的子群的代数性判定，并解答了Popov 2005年提出的问题，同时刻画了多项式控制系统可精确有限维双线性实现的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机源于代数和几何中关于导子代数结构的基本问题：在有限生成交换代数上，局部有限（locally finite）导子构成的Lie子代数是否能保持有限维结构？此问题连接了Lie代数理论、代数群理论与控制理论多个领域，特别是Popov在2005年提出的关于单参酉子群生成的子群何时代数的问题，需要一个统一的框架来解决。

Method: 论文引入了关键的新技术：构造与原环的理想cofinite相交的ide的closure meeting every associated point of Spec A。在满足限制（对相应有限子概形沿二阶消失）的导子子代数上，局部有限性被证明迫使其退化为局部幂零性。应用这一事实，将L与此子代数的交集用第二个定理（局部幂零Lie子代数的可解性）处理，得到有限余维，再结合伴随作用的局部有限性推导出L的有限维性。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 有限生成Lie子代数L⊆Der(A)，若所有元素在A上局部有限，则L有限维；(2) 有限个局部有限导子生成的Lie子代数，其有限维性、局部有限作用、所有元素局部有限三者等价；(3) 元素全为局部幂零的Lie子代数可解，且约化情形下导长度≤dim A（界最优）；(4) 对仿射簇X，Aut(X)中有限个连通代数子群生成的子群是代数的当且仅当其切代数生成的Lie代数中每个元素局部有限；(5) 回答了Popov 2005年的问题；(6) 刻画了多项式控制系统可精确有限维双线性实现的充要条件。

Conclusion: 本文通过构造cofinite ideal的技术将局部有限性"压缩"为局部幂零性，建立了从局部有限导子到有限维Lie代数的桥梁，统一解决了代数、几何和控制论中的多个相关问题。该方法不仅证明了有限维性结果，还给出了局部幂零Lie子代数可解性的最佳界，展现了该技术路径的深远潜力。

Abstract: Let $A$ be a finitely generated commutative algebra over a field $\mathbb K$ of characteristic zero. We prove that every finitely generated Lie subalgebra $L\subseteq\operatorname{Der}_{\mathbb K}(A)$ whose elements are locally finite on $A$ is finite-dimensional. Consequently, for a Lie subalgebra generated by finitely many locally finite derivations, the following are equivalent: it is finite-dimensional, it acts locally finitely on $A$, and all its elements are locally finite. A key ingredient is a second theorem of independent interest: every Lie subalgebra of $\operatorname{Der}_{\mathbb K}(A)$ whose elements are locally nilpotent is solvable; when $A$ is reduced, its derived length is at most $\dim A$, and this bound is sharp. For an affine variety $X$ over an algebraically closed field, we deduce that a subgroup of $\operatorname{Aut}(X)$ generated by finitely many connected algebraic subgroups is algebraic if and only if every element of the Lie algebra generated by their tangent algebras is locally finite. For two unipotent one-parameter subgroups, this provides an answer to a problem posed by Popov in 2005. Our results also characterize polynomial control systems admitting an exact finite-dimensional bilinear realization by polynomial observables containing the state coordinates. The proofs rest on the introduction of a cofinite ideal meeting the closure of every associated point of $\operatorname{Spec} A$: on the subalgebra of derivations vanishing to second order along the corresponding finite subscheme, local finiteness forces local nilpotence. The intersection of that subalgebra with $L$ is therefore solvable by the second theorem, and has finite codimension in $L$; together with local finiteness of the adjoint action, this yields finite-dimensionality.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用对数几何技术（将对数结构扩展到非有限生成幺半群情形），建立tame log étale基本群的类似有限性陈述，并利用adic空间之间的"垂直紧化"方法完成证明。

Conclusion: Tame étale基本群的有限生成/有限展示性质在广泛的非archimedean设置下成立，对数几何和垂直紧化是处理该问题的关键技术工具。

Abstract: We introduce the tame étale fundamental group $π_1^t(X/K)$ of a rigid space X over a non-archimedean field K. We show that if X is qcqs and K has topologically finitely generated tame Galois group (e.g. algebraically closed or a local field), then $π_1^t(X/K)$ is topologically finitely generated. If X is moreover the rigid generic fibre of a strictly semistable formal scheme such that the smooth locus of its special fibre admits a projective snc compactification, then $π_1^t(X/K)$ is topologically finitely presented. The proofs rely on techniques of logarithmic geometry (extended beyond its usual scope of finitely generated monoids), in particular on an analogous finiteness statement for the tame log étale fundamental group, and on the 'vertical compactification' of a map of adic spaces.

</details>

Abstract: In 1972, M. Sato proposed an approach to proving modularity of forms like Thetanullwerte by characterizing them via certain differential operators of infinite order (DOI) in the modular variable(s) alone. A DOI is an infinite series in derivatives decreasing so fast that it acts on holomorphic functions by a sheaf morphism. This approach was developed by several authors including Kashiwara, Kawai, Takei and Yoshida.
  We give an interpretation of this approach using supersymmetry which provides a natural source of DOIs: the naive exponential of any odd supersymmetry generator is a DOI. The case of the Riemann theta function of genus n is governed by the supergroup OSp(1|2n) (and its metaplectic cover) acting on a natural super-thickening of the Siegel plane. For n=2 this is the 3-dimensional N=1 superconformal group and the structure at hand is precisely the free massless scalar supermultiplet (combining the Laplace and Dirac equations). For n>2 we get a super-extension of the generalized conformal structure existing on the Lagrangian Grassmannian as on any Hermitian symmetric space.
  An additional interesting feature here is that the odd supersymmetry generators acting ``on-shell'' (i.e., in the space of solutions of the equations of motion) satisfy even-style Heisenberg commutation relations. These equations of motion upgrade to a complex of differential operators corresponding to a natural BGG-type resolution of the super-Weil representation of osp(1|2n).

</details>

Abstract: Let $Y$ be a very general prime Fano threefold of genus 7. We exhibit an explicit 2-cycle on $Y\times Y$ that is Abel-Jacobi trivial but non-torsion in the Chow group $A^4(Y\times Y)$. Abstract: This paper develops a phase-plane formulation of gravitational light deflection by static and spherically symmetric black holes, with Schwarzschild spacetime as the principal case. Instead of perturbing the null trajectory and locating the displaced outgoing asymptote, we represent the orbit through an amplitude and an intrinsic phase. The bending angle then follows from the excess physical azimuth accumulated while the intrinsic phase advances between two fixed asymptotic endpoints. For Schwarzschild spacetime, the exact radial first integral reduces the amplitude evolution to a cubic algebraic relation. Its physical branch generates the local phase factor through a single inverse algebraic map. Lagrange inversion then yields an explicit all-order weak-deflection coefficient formula in powers of the invariant ratio \(M/b\). Each coefficient separates into an algebraic phase contribution and a universal trigonometric moment, which explains the alternating rational and \(π\)-dependent structure of the Schwarzschild series. Independent comparison with the exact radial scattering integral and conventional orbit perturbation reproduces the standard weak-bending coefficients. The same phase framework extends to general static spherical geometries, while the Schwarzschild cubic represents an especially simple member of a broader algebraic class. The branch singularity of the phase map also coincides with the critical photon orbit and governs the convergence of the weak-deflection expansion.

The resulting conformally flat, constant-mean-curvature ADM initial slice satisfies the Hamiltonian and momentum constraints, with the residuals converging under resolution refinement. The geometry approaches Reissner-Nordström at large radius, reduces to the neutral zero-point-length solution for $q=0$, and coincides geometrically with the Ayón-Beato-García solution for $\ell=|q|$. The center is regular and is de Sitter, locally Minkowski, or anti-de Sitter according to the sign of $2M\ell-q^2$; the weak energy condition holds globally if and only if $3M\ell\geq2q^2$. We derive the extremality curve, the exact heat capacity, and homogeneous horizon-variation and Smarr identities while retaining the Wald area entropy. We also prove that every charged black hole in this family has a nondegenerate extraordinary NED optical metric throughout the domain of outer communication. The associated capture shadow is selected by the global minimum of the optical impact-parameter function and generally differs from the background-geodesic shadow. Weak-field calculations yield the periapsis, bending, time-delay, and redshift corrections; in particular, $\ell$ first appears beyond the standard first-post-Newtonian parameters. Finally, for minimally coupled test radiation in a cold transparent plasma, we obtain exact parametric shadow relations for power-law density profiles and combine Hamiltonian ray tracing with a Novikov-Thorne disk model. Abstract: We extend the recently proposed modified double-copy formalism to quasitopological gravity (QTG) coupled to matter. The nonlinear electrodynamics governing this auxiliary field is determined entirely by the generating function $h(p)$ that specifies the QTG model, while the corresponding current is determined by the matter stress-energy tensor. Restricting the auxiliary solution to a $D$-dimensional hyperplane and applying the modified double-copy prescription yields the Kerr--Schild metric solving the QTG equations. In the absence of null currents, the resulting solutions satisfy a generalized Birkhoff theorem and are static, whereas null charged currents naturally generate Vaidya-type solutions. Abstract: We formulate a horizon-regular double-null criterion for regular inner marginal horizons in spherically symmetric metric $f(R)$ gravity. If $\mathcal{P}_{\ell n}\leq F/r^2$ along a regular ingoing null segment issuing from a nondegenerate future outer marginal sphere, then the outgoing expansion cannot return to zero, and no second regular marginal sphere of the same family can occur on that generator. Conversely, a nondegenerate future inner marginal sphere requires the reverse inequality, so an outer--inner pair necessarily entails a source reversal and an exact integral balance. No trapped-region assumption is required. In the static limit, the criterion reduces to a horizon-regular relation involving the radial derivative of the metric function and remains valid in the degenerate case under the stated regularity conditions. It reproduces the Reissner--Nordström classification and is verified in an exact charged, nonconstant-curvature $f(R)$ black hole with a nonconstant scalaron. The resulting Cauchy-horizon statement is conditional and applies only when the candidate boundary is also a regular nondegenerate future inner marginal horizon.

</details>

Abstract: In this work, we establish a systematic framework to describe the vacuum decays in a radiation-dominated FLRW universe from the Schwinger-Keldysh formalism. As a phenomenological example, we consider a polynomial potential and study the structure of the memory kernel as well as the correlation functions of the noise term. The resulting charged anti-de Sitter (AdS) and de Sitter (dS) solution is gauge independent and reduces to the Reissner--Nordström--AdS (RN-AdS) black hole when the regularization parameter vanishes. Electric charge and a cosmological constant preserve the Minkowski core: the metric approaches the Minkowski geometry at the center, the Kretschmann scalar vanishes there, and the spacetime exhibits a multi-horizon structure. Focusing on AdS backgrounds, we investigate the black hole thermodynamics. For a regularization parameter below a critical value, the model exhibits a phase transition with consistent signatures across these thermodynamic quantities, demonstrating that Minkowski-core regularization can preserve center regularity while modifying the AdS thermodynamic phase structure relative to the RN-AdS benchmark.

Abstract: We investigated the thermodynamic and quantum-information cost of irreversible information erasure at the event horizon of hairy black holes generated through extended geometric deformation. Starting from the Schwarzschild black hole seed solution, we considered two families of hairy geometries satisfying the strong and dominant energy conditions. Lan-dauer's principle was used to relate the minimum energy required to erase one bit of information to the Hawking temperature of deformed horizon. We derived corresponding horizon radii, Hawking temperatures, Bekenstein-Hawking entropies and normalised Landauer costs as functions of decoupling parameter, the hair length scale and the effective charge associated with the additional gravitational sector. For the parameter ranges analyzed, our results show that gravitational hair tends to reduce the Hawking temperature and the minimum Landauer erasure cost while simultaneously increasing the horizon area and the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy. The Reeb-Wolf finite-size correction provides an additional positive contribution to the erasure cost but remains subdominant due to the large effective dimension of the horizon reservoir. We further separated the Reeb-Wolf correction into mutual-information and relative-entropy contributions, showing that the former is governed by the area-spacing parameter, whereas the latter is controlled by the actual energy gap between neighboring horizon levels. Furthermore, the Landauer spectrum obtained by quantizing the area decreases as the quantum number associated with the horizon increases, although this variation does not occur in the same way in the SEC and DEC branches. Overall, the presence of gravitational hair alters the thermodynamic properties of the horizon and, consequently, the Landauer cost associated with information erasure, both in the continuous regime and after quantizing the area.

We show that assuming a non-zero temperature effectively results in a larger radius while leaving the total mass of the star essentially unchanged, and discuss the interplay between the effective gravitational constant, central density, and radius of the star.

Abstract: We develop an efficient three-dimensional numerical-relativity framework for primordial black-hole (PBH) formation from superhorizon curvature perturbations in a radiation-dominated Universe. We implement flux-conservative relativistic hydrodynamics in the adaptive-mesh-refinement code \textsc{GRChombo} and introduce a cosmologically scaled Gamma-driver that allows the cosmic-time step to grow in proportion to the scale factor. For a representative long-term simulation, the scaled driver preserves the apparent-horizon mass evolution and constraint behavior while reducing the number of coarse-level advances by a factor of approximately $94$ relative to the standard driver. Applying the framework to a spherical Gaussian curvature profile, we find a collapse threshold $0.79578 < μ_c < 0.79580$ and a critical exponent $γ\simeq 0.3559$, consistent with previous spherically symmetric results. We further fit the late-time PBH mass growth to the Zel'dovich--Novikov accretion law, demonstrating that the code can follow both near-critical collapse and long-term post-formation evolution in three dimensions. The framework provides a foundation for future studies of PBH formation beyond spherical symmetry.

Abstract: We study static, spherically symmetric white dwarfs in minimal dilatonic gravity (MDG) -- a Brans-Dicke theory with fixed coupling $α^2=1/3 $ and free Compton length $λ_Φ$. The ratio by which MDG reduces the maximum mass is robust against the equation of state. Including rigid rotation near mass shedding, consistency with the most massive observed white dwarfs restricts $λ_Φ \lesssim 300$ km. An independent gravitational-redshift bound gives $λ_Φ \lesssim 720$ km. Because the dilaton mass is density-independent the same coupling produces a percent-level altitude dependence of the Kepler-inferred $ GM_\oplus$, constraining it from the Solar-System side. To tackle the problem, we introduce a free-boundary collocation method with a linearized-exterior Robin condition that eliminates the exponential stiffness of shooting methods and gives access to the screened regime in double precision.

Abstract: Gravitational-wave standard sirens provide a direct measurement of luminosity distance and therefore offer a new way to test gravity over cosmological scales. By contrast, the Lorentz-violating parameter $\ell_0$ remains prior dominated, and the evolution index $β$ is constrained only by the gravitational-wave sector. The best forecast precision, $Δ\ell_0\simeq0.028$, is about $4.7\times10^{12}$ times weaker than the bound implied by GW170817. The principal result is therefore a quantified sensitivity gap rather than a forecast detection. We also express the prediction in the phenomenological $(Ξ,n)$ description of modified gravitational-wave propagation, allowing direct comparison with standard-siren studies of other gravity models.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 曲率依赖的共形变换在修正引力（尤其是 $f(R)$ 引力）的框架变换中扮演关键角色，但以往文献通常假设这种映射在度量变量空间中具有局域可逆性。作者旨在严格检验这一假设，明确区分"前向映射的局部性"与"逆映射的局部性"，为评估有效作用量、半经典分析和量子框架等价性提供精确框架。

Method: 作者引入独立辅助标量来局域化前向映射，同时指出恢复原始度量需满足微分约束。通过计算完整的逆度量切映射，发现其包含非多项式投影子 $\xi^\mu\xi^\nu/\xi^2$，从而证明不存在可微有限喷射逆。随后以度量 $f(R)$ 引力为例，将爱因斯坦框架的标量-张量表示视为母理论，推导拉回经典 Hessian、壳外嵌入项，并在二次模型中通过约束高斯消元展示标量场非局域核、法向行列式与零模条件。

Result: 证明了：(1) 曲率依赖的共形映射在无约束度量上不存在局域（有限喷射）逆映射；(2) 但在指定边界或柯西数据后存在分支性泛函逆；(3) 精确的母理论与度量解保持等价；(4) 障碍在于局域性以及壳外变分和涨落域，而非解本身的等价性；(5) 二次模型中法向行列式与零模相容性条件被显式导出。

Conclusion: 该研究为曲率依赖的框架变换提供了精确的数学框架，澄清了此类变换在量子层面可能破坏局域性和壳外等价性的本质原因，对修正引力的有效作用量计算和量子框架等价性研究具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Curvature-dependent conformal rules are local forward assignments on known metrics, but they are not generically local changes of metric variables. We study the nondegenerate class $\widetilde g_{μν}=F(R[g])g_{μν}$, with $F>0$ and $F_R\neq0$. Introducing an independent auxiliary scalar localizes the forward map, while recovering the original metric requires a differential constraint. The complete inverse metric tangent map contains the nonpolynomial projector $ξ^μξ^ν/ξ^2$; hence no differentiable finite-jet inverse, i.e., a formula involving only finitely many derivatives at the same point, exists on an open set of unrestricted metrics. Branchwise functional inverses may nevertheless exist after boundary or Cauchy data are specified. Metric $f(R)$ gravity gives an explicit realization: its local Einstein-frame scalar--tensor representation is a parent theory, whereas a metric-only Einstein-side description requires a differential section governed by a normal operator. We derive the pulled-back classical Hessian and its off-shell embedding term, and in the quadratic model show how constrained Gaussian elimination produces the scalaron nonlocal kernel, the corresponding normal determinant for the displayed measure, and the zero-mode compatibility condition. Exact parent and metric solutions remain equivalent; the obstruction concerns locality and the off-shell variational and fluctuation domains. These results provide a precise framework for assessing curvature-dependent frame transformations in modified gravity and clarify their implications for effective actions, semiclassical analyses, and quantum frame equivalence.

</details>


### [95] [Quantum-Corrected Thermodynamics and Phase Structure of AdS Euler-Heisenberg Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13378)
*Siddhartha Sankar Borah,Dhruba Jyoti Gogoi,Kalyan Bhuyan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于非线性电动力学修正（爱因斯坦-欧拉-海森堡框架）的AdS黑洞，在扩展相空间中引入热涨落（量子效应）后的热力学行为。熵获得了对数和逆面积修正，焓、内能、亥姆霍兹自由能和吉布斯自由能均被修正，比热出现多重发散和符号变化，表明真实的二级相变以及量子稳定微观相后跟随着普适宏观不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非线性电动力学修正引力下AdS黑洞的热力学性质，特别是量子涨落（热涨落）如何改变黑洞的相空间结构和稳定性，以揭示量子修正对黑洞热力学的主导作用。

Method: 从爱因斯坦-欧拉-海森堡框架出发构造修正黑洞解，在扩展相空间中分析热力学性质；通过对熵引入对数及逆面积量子修正项，推导修正后的焓、内能、亥姆霍兹自由能和吉布斯自由能，并计算修正比热以分析相变行为。

Result: 比热在修正后出现多重发散和符号改变，证实存在真实的二级相变；观测到由量子效应稳定的微观相以及后续的普适宏观不稳定性；热涨落从根本上重构了热力学相空间。

Conclusion: 热涨落的量子效应显著改变AdS黑洞的相结构，量子修正对黑洞稳定性起主导作用，为非线性电动力学修正引力体系中黑洞相变提供了新的理论洞见。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic behaviour of an AdS black hole arising from nonlinear electrodynamics-corrected gravity, incorporating quantum effects through thermal fluctuations. From the Einstein--Euler--Heisenberg framework, we consider the modified black hole solution and analyse its thermodynamic properties in the extended phase space. Logarithmic and inverse-area corrections to the entropy are obtained, leading to modified expressions for enthalpy, internal energy, Helmholtz free energy and Gibbs free energy. The corrected specific heat exhibits multiple divergences and sign changes, signalling genuine second-order phase transitions and revealing a quantum-stabilized microscopic phase followed by universal macroscopic instability. Our results demonstrate that thermal fluctuations qualitatively restructure the thermodynamic phase space and highlight the dominant role of quantum corrections in governing the black hole stability.

</details>


### [96] [Quadratic effective energy--momentum tensor on uniform-density hypersurfaces during slow-roll inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13498)
*Inyong Cho*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了慢滚暴胀期间，在均匀密度超曲面上标量宇宙学微扰的二次阶有效能量-动量张量（2EMT）。2EMT由线性度规和暴胀子微扰的二次项构成，是规范固定的有效源而非规范不变观测量。作者施加了完整标量规范条件δρ=0和E=0，用Bardeen势Ψ表达所有微扰，并在长、短波长区域评估傅里叶空间中的2EMT。严格红外极限下均匀密度与共动结果一致；中等红外区域主导阶相同但有限梯度修正区分两规范；紫外区域2EMT因缓变的物质时钟ρ0'∝ε受到1/ε增强，且均匀密度的主导项因拉普拉斯项额外受到1/σ2²增强。与纵向、空间平坦和共动表达式的对比揭示了规范依赖的结构性：均匀密度与共动切片在绝热超哈勃模上一致，而纵向和空间平坦规范在严格红外下受慢滚抑制，在中等红外下成为梯度主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解宇宙学微扰论中有效能量-动量张量（2EMT）的规范依赖性是必要的，因为在慢滚暴胀背景下，二次阶有效源并非规范不变的观测量。作者旨在系统评估均匀密度规范下的2EMT，并与其它规范（纵向、空间平坦、共动）进行对比，以厘清各规范之间在红外和紫外区域的结构性差异。

Method: 作者在均匀密度切片上施加完整标量规范条件δρ=0和E=0，将所有标量微扰用Bardeen势Ψ表示，并在傅里叶空间中计算二次阶有效能量-动量张量。分析区分了严格红外/紫外极限与中间区域，并分别对均匀密度、共动、纵向和空间平坦规范的结果进行显式比较。

Result: 严格红外极限下均匀密度与共动结果一致；中等红外区域主导阶相同但有限梯度修正区分两种规范。紫外区域2EMT受1/ε增强（来自缓变物质时钟ρ0'∝ε），均匀密度主导项额外受1/σ2²增强（来自拉普拉斯项）。对比显示纵向和空间平坦规范在严格红外下受慢滚参数抑制，在中等红外下变为梯度主导。

Conclusion: 2EMT的规范依赖具有清晰的结构：均匀密度与共动切片对绝热超哈勃模保持一致，而纵向和空间平坦规范在红外极限下受到慢滚抑制并在中等红外区域表现出梯度主导行为。均匀密度规范在紫外区域的增强效应源于其时钟的缓变特性及拉普拉斯项的贡献。

Abstract: We investigate the quadratic-order effective energy--momentum tensor (2EMT) of scalar cosmological perturbations on uniform-density hypersurfaces during slow-roll inflation. The 2EMT is constructed from terms quadratic in the linear metric and inflaton perturbations, and is therefore a gauge-fixed effective source rather than a gauge-invariant observable. We impose the complete scalar gauge conditions $δρ=0$ and $E=0$, express all perturbations in terms of the Bardeen potential $Ψ$, and evaluate the Fourier-space 2EMT in the long- and short-wavelength domains. We distinguish the ``strict'' infrared and ultraviolet limits from the ``intermediate'' regimes. The uniform-density and comoving results agree in the strict infrared limit. In the intermediate infrared regime, the dominant leading order remains the same, while explicit finite-gradient corrections distinguish the two gauges. In the ultraviolet, the 2EMT is enhanced by $1/ε$ due to the slowly varying matter clock, $ρ_0'\proptoε$, and the leading uniform-density 2EMT terms exhibit an additional enhancement by $1/σ_2^2$ $(σ_2\equiv \cal{H}/k)$ from the Laplacian term. The intermediate ultraviolet expansion makes the subleading gradient hierarchy explicit without changing the leading terms. We compare these results with newly recalculated longitudinal, spatially-flat, and comoving expressions, displayed in a more explicit form than in the earlier analysis. The comparison shows that the gauge dependence is structured: uniform-density and comoving slicings coincide for adiabatic super-Hubble modes, whereas the longitudinal and spatially-flat gauges are {\it slow-roll} suppressed in the strict infrared and become {\it gradient} dominated in the intermediate infrared.

</details>


### [97] [Vector Perturbations in Ghost-Free Quasidilaton Massive Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13529)
*Ekapob Kulchoakrungsun,Daris Samart*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了ghost-free extended quasidilaton massive gravity中横向矢量微扰在存在最小物质（标量场与Abel矢量场）时的行为，结论是添加最小物质无法修复真空情形下自加速分支上矢量模式的运动学系数为零（强耦合）的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究已发现，在没有quasidilaton动能项的情况下，自加速分支（Branch II, J=0）上引力矢量模式的动能系数K_V为零，导致矢量模式在线性阶上无限强耦合。自然的问题是：引入普通最小物质（如标量场、Maxwell或Proca场）是否能改变这一结论，使得矢量扇区变得可正常微扰展开。

Method: 在ghost-free extended quasidilaton massive gravity（不含quasidilaton动能项）框架下，加入一个canonical标量场和单个Abelian矢量场（Maxwell或Proca）。对辅助shift自由度做整合消去，比较引入物质前后K_V的表达式；同时利用Friedmann方程简化约束；分析矢量物质场在均匀各向同性背景下能否与引力矢量模发生二次阶耦合。

Result: （1）引入标量场与Abel矢量场后，整合消去辅助shift，得到的K_V与真空情形完全一致，仍为零；（2）标量物质没有横向扰动，其进入未化简的shift约束的项在利用Friedmann方程后相互抵消；（3）具有零各向同性背景的Maxwell或Proca场在二次阶不与引力矢量模混合。因此最小物质仍无法使Branch II上矢量扇区变得可正常微扰。

Conclusion: 普通最小物质不足以修复自加速分支（Branch II）上引力矢量模式的线性级强耦合问题。若需要在线性级获得健康的引力矢量模式，应使用Branch I。

Abstract: We study transverse vector perturbations in ghost-free extended quasidilaton massive gravity without a quasidilaton kinetic term, in the presence of minimal matter. In vacuum, we recover the known result that the kinetic coefficient $K_V$ of the gravitational vector modes vanishes on the self-accelerating branch $J=0$, so those modes are infinitely strongly coupled at linear order. We then add a canonical scalar field and a single Abelian vector (Maxwell or Proca). After integrating out the auxiliary shift, we find the same $K_V$ as in vacuum. The scalar matter has no transverse perturbation; it enters the unsimplified shift constraint, but those terms cancel once the Friedmann equation is used. A Maxwell or Proca field with vanishing isotropic background does not mix with the gravitational vectors at quadratic order. Minimal matter therefore leaves $K_V=0$ on Branch II. We do not claim that the modes are absent from the nonlinear theory. We do conclude that ordinary minimal matter is not enough to make the vector sector perturbatively healthy on this branch. If we need healthy gravitational vector modes at the linear level, Branch I is the branch to use.

</details>

### [98] [Distance Matrices of Ordered Point Clouds and Their Persistent Homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12620)
*David Hien*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该论文建立了时间序列的距离矩阵过滤（即递推图）与其状态空间嵌入的Čech/Vietoris-Rips过滤之间的度一链映射关系，研究了同调诱导映射的性质，并将这些链映射应用于简化图像持久性计算，用于分析不同类型的周期运动之间的转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列的距离矩阵可以可视化为热图，其子水平集即递推图，在时间序列分析中广泛使用。作者希望从拓扑数据分化的视角，在距离矩阵过滤与状态空间嵌入过滤之间建立严格的数学联系，从而为递推图和应用拓扑方法提供理论基础。

Method: 论文在时间序列的距离矩阵过滤与状态空间嵌入的Čech（或Vietoris-Rips）过滤之间构造了一个度一链映射（degree-one chain map），并研究该链映射在同调水平上诱导的映射。具体而言，论证了从H_0到H_1的诱导映射本质上是满射（essentially surjective），并给出一个从H_1到H_2的映射非平凡的实例。这些链映射还被应用于简化循环签名（cycling signatures）计算中的图像持久性（image persistence）计算。

Result: 建立了距离矩阵过滤到状态空间嵌入过滤的度一链映射；证明了H_0到H_1的诱导映射本质上是满射，并给出H_1到H_2映射非平凡的例子；该方法能简化循环签名计算中的图像持久性计算，并产生更精细的信息以分析不同类型循环运动之间的转换。

Conclusion: 该工作桥接了递推图分析与状态空间嵌入拓扑之间的理论间隙，为时间序列的拓扑分析提供了统一的链复形框架，并展示了其在循环运动转换分析中的实际价值。

Abstract: The distance matrix of a finite point cloud can be visualized as a heatmap. When the data arise from a time series, the sublevel sets of this image are known as recurrence plots and are widely used in time series analysis. Motivated by this perspective, we establish a relationship between the distance-matrix filtration of the time series and the Čech (or Vietoris--Rips) filtration of its state-space embedding in the form of a degree-one chain map. We study the induced maps in homology, showing that the map from $H_0$ into $H_1$ is essentially surjective and providing an example where the map from $H_1$ into $H_2$ is nontrivial. These chain maps can be applied to simplify image persistence computations arising in the computation of cycling signatures, a topological tool for time series analysis. Moreover, these computations yield finer information that allows the analysis of transitions between different types of cycling motion.

</details>

### [99] [Categorification of the genus two DAHA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13278)
*Semeon Arthamonov,Ievgen Makedonskyi,Daniil Sarafannikov*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在平坦退化D(2|1,α)的当前代数的导出范畴上构造了三个导出自函子，并证明了它们的Grothendieck群中的类恰为亏格2的Macdonald算子。通过显式计算分次Ext配对，推导出亏格2 Macdonald算子的自伴随性以及亏格2 Macdonald多项式关于特定斜双线性形式的正交性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在将Macdonald算子（对称函数理论中的核心对象）推广到亏格2（超椭圆）情形，并通过表示论方法建立其内在的代数结构。利用李代数的导出范畴和Grothendieck群为工具，探索这些高阶Macdonald算子与表示论之间的深层联系。

Method: 作者构造了三个导出自函子作用在平坦退化D(2|1,α)当前代数的分次可积表示的导出范畴上，通过类映射将其对应到Grothendieck群中的元素，证明这些元素正是亏格2的Macdonald算子。随后，通过显式计算分次Ext配对来推导算子的自伴随性质及多项式的正交性。

Result: 成功证明了亏格2 Macdonald算子在Grothendieck群中的表示论实现，并获得了亏格2 Macdonald算子的自伴随性以及亏格2 Macdonald多项式在特定斜双线性形式下的正交性结果。

Conclusion: 该工作将表示论与亏格2 Macdonald理论建立了桥梁，推广了经典亏格1情形下已知的表示论方法，为高阶Macdonald多项式的研究提供了新的代数框架。

Abstract: We construct three derived endofunctors on the derived category of graded integrable representations of the current algebra of a flat degeneration of $D(2|1,α)$, and prove that their classes in the Grothendieck group are the genus two Macdonald operators. Computing the graded $\operatorname{Ext}$ pairing explicitly, we deduce the self-adjointness of the genus two Macdonald operators and the orthogonality of genus $2$ Macdonald polynomials with respect to the certain skew-bilinear form.

</details>


### [100] [Nilpotent orbits of sl(n,R) admit no Gibbs state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12608)
*Jérémie Pierard de Maujouy,Guillaume Neuttiens*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了SL(n,R)的幂零轨道上的Gibbs态，证明了当n>2时，这些轨道不存在任何Gibbs态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs态是定义在Hamiltonian G-流形上的自然平衡态，由李代数g中的开集参数化。理解哪些几何对象（如幂零轨道）能承载Gibbs态，对于理解系统的平衡行为至关重要。

Method: 本文聚焦于SL(n,R)的幂零轨道，通过分析这些轨道的几何和拓扑性质，证明了对于n>2的情形，幂零轨道无法满足Gibbs态存在的必要条件。

Result: 证明了对于n>2，SL(n,R)的所有幂零轨道都不存在任何Gibbs态。

Conclusion: 幂零轨道在n>2时不能作为Gibbs态所依赖的平衡态载体，这揭示了SL(n,R)幂零轨道与Gibbs态理论之间深刻的不相容性。

Abstract: Gibbs states are probability distributions defined on Hamiltonian G-manifolds. They are parametrized by an open set of the Lie algebra g and arise as natural equilibrium states with regard to the action of G on the system. In this paper, we focus on nilpotent orbits of SL(n,R). We show that for n>2, those orbits do not admit any Gibbs state.

</details>


### [101] [Langlands Duality and Invariant Differential Operators: the Case SL(2n+1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13400)
*V. K. Dobrev*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文构建了Langlands对偶与不变微分算子之间的联系，针对SL(2n+1)群情形展开研究，探索其表示理论与不变微分算子之间的深层关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Langlands纲领是数学研究中最具影响力的课题之一，而不变微分算子领域看似与之无关，但两者都根植于Harish-Chandra的半单李群表示理论。作者旨在建立这两者之间的桥梁，先前已研究了SL(2n)情形，本文进一步拓展到SL(2n+1)情形。

Method: 作者首先从SL(2n)群的情形出发建立了初步联系，本文将其推广至SL(2n+1)群。尽管这两个群在结构上相似，但它们的表示理论存在显著差异，作者针对这一差异进行了专门处理和分析。

Result: 由于本文为摘要性质，尚未呈现具体结果，但明确指出SL(2n)与SL(2n+1)两个群虽相似，其表示理论却相当不同，这构成了研究的核心难点与创新点。

Conclusion: 本文是建立Langlands对偶与不变微分算子之间桥梁系列工作的一部分，针对SL(2n+1)情形展开研究，为后续深入的理论构建奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recently we started building a bridge between two cases of Langlands duality. The latter is one of the most influential topics in mathematical research. It has many different appearances and influential subtopics. Yet there is a topic that until now seems unrelated to the Langlands program. That is the topic of invariant differential operators. That is strange since both items are deeply rooted in Harish-Chandra's representation theory of semisimple Lie groups.
  We started with the case of the group ~$SL(2n)$. In the present paper we deal with the group $SL(2n+1)$. The two mentioned groups are similar, but their representation theories is rather different.

</details>


### [102] [Minimal Nilpotent Orbits of type G2, F4 and E8](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12985)
*Boming Jia*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于复单李代数类型G₂、F₄和E₈，其最小幂零轨道的闭包不能同构于任何光滑拟仿射簇X的余切丛的仿射化(T^*X)^aff，且此结论不要求同构是等变的或泊松的。该结论在类型A₁和A₂中同样成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟仿射簇的余切丛的仿射化（(T^*X)^aff）是代数几何中的一类重要对象。研究最小幂零轨道闭包是否可表示为这种形式，有助于理解幂零轨道闭包的几何结构，并将其与更一般的余切丛仿射化类进行区分。该问题的非等变、非泊松情形此前未被完全解决，本文填补了这一空白。

Method: 作者针对复单李代数的例外类型G₂、F₄、E₈以及经典类型A₁、A₂，通过深入分析这些最小幂零轨道闭包的几何与奇异性性质，建立了与(T^*X)^aff形式的不兼容性论证。证明无需假设同构具有等变性或保持泊松结构，说明结论在更广泛的范畴内成立。

Result: 成功证明了在类型G₂、F₄、E₈（以及A₁、A₂）中，最小幂零轨道闭包\overline{\mathcal O}_{\min}(\mathfrak g)不同构于任何光滑拟仿射簇的余切丛仿射化(T^*X)^aff，并且该结论在非等变、非泊松的推广意义下依然成立。

Conclusion: 该结果表明这些类型的最小幂零轨道闭包具有独特的几何结构，不能由余切丛仿射化来重现，从而深化了我们对幂零轨道闭包与余切丛仿射化之间关系（即Beauville–Losev–Rowell型猜想相关主题）的理解。

Abstract: Let $\mathfrak g$ be a complex simple Lie algebra of type $G_2$, $F_4$, or $E_8$. We prove that the closure $\overline{\mathcal O}_{\min}(\mathfrak g)$ of its minimal nilpotent orbit is not isomorphic to $(T^*X)^{\mathrm{aff}}$ for any smooth quasi-affine variety $X$. We do not assume that the isomorphism is equivariant or Poisson. We also prove the same statement in types $A_1$ and $A_2$.

</details>


### [103] [Absolutely flat algebras in tensor categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13137)
*Kevin Coulembier,Alexander Sherman*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究张量范畴（刚性阿贝尔幺半范畴）的归纳完备化中半单代数、阿廷单代数与阿廷绝对平坦代数之间的等价关系，证明在中等增长对称张量范畴中（若接受领域主要猜想则适用于所有范畴），三种类型的交换代数是重合的；同时给出反例说明在一般情况下这些概念会发散，异于域上代数的经典情形。附录中还首次给出具有有限维态射空间但对象具有无限长度的张量范畴例子，并推广了Deligne关于坦纳克范畴在代数闭域上零化的定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典代数几何中，域上代数的半单性、阿廷单代数与阿廷绝对平坦性之间存在紧密联系。作者希望将这些经典概念推广到更一般的张量范畴框架中，探究在何种条件下这些代数概念仍然保持一致，从而深化对张量范畴内部结构的理解。

Method: 通过系统地比较半单代数、阿廷单代数和阿廷绝对平坦代数在张量范畴归纳完备化中的定义与性质，限制在中等增长的对称张量范畴中进行研究，利用Deligne定理及其推广作为核心工具，并构造具体反例说明一般情况下的差异。

Result: 在中等增长对称张量范畴中三类交换代数是重合的（若接受领域主要猜想则对所有范畴成立）；同时构造例子表明在一般情况下这些概念会分离。附录一提供了首个具有有限维态射空间但对象无限长的张量范畴例子；附录二将Deligne定理推广为在代数闭域上坦纳克范畴是零化的。

Conclusion: 半单代数、阿廷单代数与阿廷绝对平坦代数在张量范畴中并非总能保持经典域上代数中的一致性；仅在中等增长对称张量范畴这一特定条件下三者重合。文中附录的构造与定理推广为张量范畴理论提供了新的工具和桥梁。

Abstract: We study the relation between semisimple algebras, artinian simple algebras, and artinian absolutely flat algebras in ind-completions of tensor categories (rigid abelian monoidal categories). Our main results show that the three types of algebras coincide, if we restrict to commutative algebras in certain (all, if we accept the main conjectures in the field) symmetric tensor categories of moderate growth. We also provide examples to show that in general these notions tend to diverge, contrary to the classical case of algebras over a field. In one appendix we give the first, to the best of our knowledge, example of a tensor category with finite-dimensional morphism spaces but objects of infinite length. In a second appendix we establish a generalisation of Deligne's theorem that tannakian categories are neutral over algebraically closed fields.

</details>


### [104] [$g$-vector fans and picture categories for 0-Auslander extriangulated categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13175)
*Erlend D. Børve,Maximilian Kaipel*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文推广了g-向量扇的概念，使其定义在具有投射silting对象的Hom-有限Krull-Schmidt 0-Auslander k-线性extriangulated范畴上，并证明了该g-向量扇具有由厚子范畴诱导的可容许划分，从而可定义picture范畴。作者建立了由预silting对象生成的厚子范畴与τ-垂直子范畴之间的双射，统一了之前所有picture范畴和τ-簇态射范畴的构造，并引入划分扇的态射为不同代数和范畴的picture范畴之间的函子关系提供统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前g-向量扇和picture范畴的概念仅适用于有限维代数的特殊情形，需要将它们统一推广到更一般的0-Auslander extriangulated范畴上，以建立不同构造之间的统一理论框架。

Method: 将g-向量扇推广到Hom-有限Krull-Schmidt 0-Auslander k-线性extriangulated范畴，利用可容许划分概念（由厚子范畴诱导）定义picture范畴；通过建立厚子范畴与τ-垂直子范畴的双射来统一已有构造；引入划分扇的态射以建立不同picture范畴之间的函子关系。

Result: 建立了投影siling对象决定的g-向量扇及其可容许划分；证明了由预silting对象生成且包含所有投射-内射对象的厚子范畴与T的端元k-代数的τ-垂直子范畴之间的一一双射；统一了有限维代数上所有已知的picture范畴和τ-簇态射范畴的构造；引入划分扇态射作为统一框架。

Conclusion: 该工作为picture范畴提供了更一般的范畴论框架，统一了先前有限维代数情形的所有构造，并通过划分扇的态射建立了不同语境间picture范畴的函子关系。

Abstract: We extend the notion of $\mathbf{g}$-vector fan so that it is defined for a Hom-finite Krull-Schmidt 0-Auslander $k$-linear extriangulated category $\mathcal{C}$ with a projective silting object $T$. Moreover, we show that the $\mathbf{g}$-vector fan admits an admissible partition, in the sense of the second-named author, which is induced by thick subcategories. One can thus define the picture category of $\mathcal{C}$. We establish a bijection between thick subcategories of $\mathcal{C}$ generated by presilting objects containing all projective-injective objects and $τ$-perpendicular subcategories of the endomorphism $k$-algebra of $T$. This shows that our construction unifies all previous constructions of picture categories and $τ$-cluster morphism categories of finite-dimensional algebras. We introduce morphisms of partitioned fans to provide a common framework for the functorial relationships between picture categories of different algebras and categories.

</details>


### [105] [Homogeneous Weights on Semigroup Algebras of Finite Commutative Semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13180)
*M. H. Shahzamanian*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域上扭曲压缩半群代数的行列式理论，将Steinberg的行列式理论扩展到扭曲设置，建立了Frobenius准则，并发展了两生成扭曲压缩幺半群代数的结构理论，进而构造了Frobenius扭曲压缩半群代数上的齐次权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限半群的行列式与其半群代数的Frobenius性质密切相关，在编码理论中有重要应用。作者希望将已有的Steinberg行列式理论推广到带扭曲（twisted）的压缩半群代数上，以扩展该理论的应用范围。

Method: 首先为扭曲压缩半群代数建立Frobenius判别准则；随后发展两生成扭曲压缩幺半群代数的结构理论，分析其定义恒等式与行列式性质的关系；接着利用特征标理论构造齐次权重，并研究非Frobenius情形下主理想上的权重平均性质。

Result: 识别出若干类代数，其相关矩阵的行列式仅依赖于零-非零模式，与上循环的值无关；从而只需对平凡特征验证行列式条件即可推出所有特征情形。在适当假设下，这些半群可由少量定义恒等式完全确定。此外，构造了Frobenius扭曲压缩半群代数上的齐次权重，并在非Frobenius情形下刻画了满足平均性质的主理想族。

Conclusion: 该工作将行列式理论与齐次权重理论推广到扭曲压缩半群代数上，将经典齐次权重概念扩展到更一般的主理想族，为有限Frobenius环上的编码理论应用提供了新的工具。

Abstract: The determinant of a finite semigroup is closely related to the Frobenius property of its corresponding semigroup algebra, and it plays a significant role in coding theory applications. In this paper, we study determinants of twisted contracted semigroup algebras over finite fields. We extend Steinberg's determinant theory to this setting by establishing a Frobenius criterion for twisted contracted semigroup algebras over finite fields. We also develop a structural theory for two-generated twisted contracted monoid algebras. In particular, we identify classes for which the determinant of the associated matrix depends only on its zero--nonzero pattern, and is therefore independent of the values of the cocycle. Consequently, it suffices to verify the determinant condition for the trivial character, from which the corresponding result for all characters follows. Under suitable assumptions, these semigroups are completely determined by a small set of defining identities. Motivated by the character-theoretic construction of homogeneous weights on finite Frobenius rings, we construct homogeneous weights for Frobenius twisted contracted semigroup algebras and investigate the non-Frobenius case. In particular, we characterize the principal ideals on which the character-average weight satisfies the averaging property of homogeneous weights and thereby extend the classical notion of homogeneous weight to a prescribed family of principal ideals.

</details>


### [106] [Moduli of super-representations of quivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13375)
*Sanjay Amrutiya,Umesh Dubey*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文通过将King的几何不变量理论框架扩展到超设置中，构造了超表示的模空间；利用超一般线性群的偶数部分和奇偶移动算子，构造了在拓扑上参数化固定超维超表示的模空间，并概述了带框架箭图的超箭图簇的构造，同时将超格拉斯曼流形和超旗簇实现为几何商。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的King GIT框架推广到超表示（super-representation）的设置中，以构造超表示的模空间，填补超几何中缺乏系统模空间理论的空白。

Method: 将King的GIT商理论扩展到超设置，利用超一般线性群的偶数部分（even part）以及奇偶移动算子（parity-shifting operator）来构造模空间；对带框架的箭图在不进行加倍（without doubling）的情况下构造超箭图簇；并通过几何商的方式实现超格拉斯曼流形和超旗簇。

Result: 成功构造了超表示的模空间，该模空间在拓扑意义上参数化固定超维的超表示；构造了带框架箭图的超箭图簇；并将超格拉斯曼流形和超旗簇实现为上述框架中的几何商。

Conclusion: 本文建立的超GIT框架为超表示模空间的构造提供了系统方法，并展示了超格拉斯曼流形与超旗簇可通过该框架自然实现，为超几何与表示论的交叉研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this note, we construct moduli spaces of super-representations of quivers by extending King's Geometric Invariant Theory (GIT) framework to the super setting. Using the even part of super general linear groups and a parity-shifting operator, we construct moduli spaces that topologically parameterize super-representations of a fixed super-dimension.
  We also outline the construction of super quiver varieties for super-representations of framed quivers (without doubling); and realise super-Grassmannians and super-flag varieties as geometric quotients within this setting.

</details>
