### [107] [Dark matter in scale-invariant gravity with hidden-sector condensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12456)
*Juan P. Garcés,Jisuke Kubo,Manfred Lindner,Markus Reinig*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，将电弱标度起源、宇宙暴胀和暗物质三个看似独立的问题联系起来，基于经典尺度不变的二次引力耦合强相互作用的隐扇区。R²项实现Starobinsky暴胀，隐扇区禁闭动态生成普朗克标度并通过引力诱导的Higgs质量生成触发电弱对称破缺。作者考察了三种隐扇区实现（隐η'介子、隐矢量玻色子、带电隐π介子）作为暗物质候选，并推导了相应的暗物质和暗辐射丰度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电弱标度起源、宇宙暴胀和暗物质通常被视为超出粒子物理和宇宙学标准模型的独立问题。作者希望探索一个共同的底层框架，将这些不同问题统一在单一理论体系中解决，而非分别处理。

Method: 采用经典尺度不变的二次引力理论（含R²项）耦合强相互作用隐扇区。R²项实现Starobinsky暴胀；隐扇区禁闭动力学生成普朗克标度，并通过引力诱导的Higgs质量生成机制触发电弱对称破缺。R²标量自由度通过普遍的能量-动量张量耦合同时加热可见与隐扇区，通过引力freeze-in机制产生隐扇区态。文章考察了三种隐扇区实现方案。

Result: 针对三种隐扇区实现（隐η'介子、隐矢量玻色子、带电隐π介子作为暗物质候选），推导并给出了相应的暗物质和暗辐射遗迹丰度，展示了该统一框架在三种不同暗物质场景下的可预测性。

Conclusion: 该框架表明电弱标度、暴胀和暗物质可以由一个共同的尺度不变的二次引力加隐扇区机制统一解释，为这些标准模型之外的主要难题提供了一个统一的洞见，并产生了可检验的暗物质与暗辐射预言。

Abstract: The origin of the electroweak scale, cosmic inflation, and dark matter are often treated as independent problems beyond the Standard Models of particle physics and cosmology. In this work, we explore the possibility that they instead arise from a common underlying framework based on classically scale-invariant quadratic gravity coupled to a strongly interacting hidden sector. The $R^2$ term naturally realizes Starobinsky inflation, while confinement in the hidden sector dynamically generates the Planck scale and triggers electroweak symmetry breaking through a gravitationally induced Higgs mass generation mechanism. The scalar degree of freedom associated with the $R^2$ term subsequently reheats both the visible and hidden sectors through universal couplings to the energy-momentum tensor, leading to the gravitational freeze-in production of hidden-sector states. We investigate three representative realizations of the hidden sector in which the dark matter candidate is either a hidden $η'$ meson, a hidden vector boson, or charged hidden pions, and derive the corresponding dark matter and dark radiation relic abundances.

</details>


### [108] [Probing CP-violating top-quark dipole moments with tomographic observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12463)
*Maxim Perelstein,Minal Shaik,Taewook Youn*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出用量子层析方法从双轻子角关联中重构顶夸克对的完整自旋密度矩阵，并利用对称性识别对CP破坏敏感的层析观测量，在LHC和FCC-ee上对（色）电偶极矩算子进行灵敏度研究，构建统计最优观测器，预期新物理能标灵敏度达10-200 TeV，显著优于传统观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顶夸克对的CP破坏是探测新物理的重要窗口，但传统观测量对CP破坏算子的灵敏度有限。通过量子层析重构完整自旋密度矩阵，可利用对称性系统地识别对CP破坏敏感的观测量，从而提升对（色）电偶极矩等新物理算子的探测能力。

Method: 利用量子层析程序从双轻子角关联重构顶夸克对的自旋密度矩阵；通过对称性论证识别对CP破坏敏感的层析观测量；构建结合层析与运动学信息的最优观测量实现统计最优灵敏度；基于快速探测器模拟顶夸克对产生与双轻子衰变通道开展分析。

Result: 在FCC-ee（365 GeV, 3 ab^-1）上对电弱电偶极矩的新物理尺度95% CL灵敏度为10-15 TeV；在LHC Run 2和HL-LHC上对顶夸克色电偶极矩的灵敏度分别为40 TeV和200 TeV，均显著超过传统观测量。

Conclusion: 层析方法在搜索CP破坏方面具有显著优势，其灵敏度超越目前实验使用的传统观测量，展示了量子层析方法在顶夸克CP破坏探测中的强大潜力。

Abstract: Quantum tomography program reconstructs the full spin density matrix of top-quark pairs from dilepton angular correlations at both electron-positron and hadron colliders. We use symmetry arguments to identify tomographic observables sensitive to CP violation in top quark couplings. As a concrete example, we study the sensitivity of these observables to (chromo-)electric dipole moment operators at the LHC and the FCC-ee. We also construct the optimal observable, which combines tomographic and kinematic information to achieve statistically optimal sensitivity for each operator and production process. The analysis is based on fast detector simulation of top pair production and decay in the dilepton channel. We project a $95\%$ CL sensitivity to new-physics scales (the inverse dipole couplings) of $10-15$ TeV for the electroweak electric dipoles at the FCC-ee ($365$ GeV, $3~{\rm ab}^{-1}$) and of 40 (200) TeV for the top chromo-electric dipole moment at the LHC Run 2 (HL-LHC). This sensitivities exceed those of the traditional observables currently used by experiments to use for these operators, indicating the power of the tomographic approach to search for CP violation.

</details>


### [109] [Soft Collinear Effective Theory for Heavy QCD Axions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12467)
*Deepanshu Bisht,Sabyasachi Chakraborty,Siddhartha Karmakar,Atanu Samanta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文为重型QCD轴子建立了软共线有效理论（SCET）框架，以B→Ka过程为基准模式，研究两种低能实现下的轴子产生机制，推导了旁观者散射对手征压低过程的贡献，并给出了轴子衰变常数f_a的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重型QCD轴子的低能现象学尚未被系统研究，特别是其与强子衰变过程的相互作用机制和退化算子消除所带来的新算子效应此前未被充分探讨。作者希望通过SCET框架明确不同实现方案下的主导贡献来源及其对轴子参数空间的约束。

Method: 采用SCET方法，在两种低能实现下研究B→Ka过程：第一种假设aG~G是μ~m_b尺度下唯一的独立轴子相互作用，通过消除弱有效理论中的冗余味变导数-胶子算子，生成新的维数为7的轴子算子O_{\partial ag}，并将其匹配到SCET，推导领头阶软贡献和旁观者散射贡献的因子化表达式（涉及微扰硬核及B介子和K介子的光锥分布振幅）；第二种假设aG~G的Wilson系数在电弱尺度以上存在，通过重整化群演化和匹配诱导出直接的b→sa算子，从而产生主导的软形状因子贡献。

Result: 在第一种实现中，旁观者散射贡献与软交叠项同阶，约占软贡献的25%；在第二种实现中，软形状因子贡献占主导，而胶子旁观者项数值上次要（约6-7%）。基于两种实现分别推导了轴子衰变常数f_a的约束边界，并比较了其现象学含义。

Conclusion: 识别出了旁观者散射在稀有B介子衰变中重轴子产生过程中变得重要的条件：当低能下aG~G是唯一独立轴子相互作用时，旁观者贡献不可忽略；而当电弱尺度以上已存在该算子时，旁观者贡献次要以软形状因子为主导。两种实现给出的f_a约束存在差异，需在实验分析中区分对待。

Abstract: We develop a soft-collinear effective theory (SCET) framework for heavy QCD axion, considering two low-energy realizations and taking $B\to Ka$ as a benchmark mode. In the first realization, $aG\widetilde G$ is assumed to be the only independent axion interaction at the scale $μ\sim m_b$. We show that eliminating the redundant flavor-changing derivative-gluon operator in the weak effective theory generates a new dimension-seven axion operator identified as $\mathrm{O}_{\partial ag}$. We match this operator onto SCET and derive the corresponding leading-power soft and spectator-scattering contributions to $B\to Ka$. We obtain a factorized expression for the spectator contribution in terms of perturbative hard kernels and the $B$- and $K$-meson light-cone distribution amplitudes. The spectator contribution arises at the same order in the power expansion as the soft-overlap term and amounts to approximately $25\%$ of the soft contribution. In the second realization, the Wilson coefficient of $aG\widetilde G$ is assumed to be present above the electroweak scale. Renormalization-group evolution and matching then induce a direct $b\to sa$ operator, which subsequently results in a dominant soft form-factor contribution, whereas the gluonic spectator term turns out to be numerically subleading ($\sim 6-7\%$). We thus identify the conditions under which spectator scattering becomes relevant for heavy-axion production in rare $B$-meson decays. Finally, we derive the corresponding bounds on the axion decay constant $f_a$ for both realizations and compare their phenomenological implications.

</details>


### [110] [Defect-Mediated Conversion: Dark Matter from Cosmological Domain-Wall Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12468)
*João Paulo Pinheiro,Mohamed Younes Sassi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的暗物质产生机制"缺陷介导转化"（DMC）：宇宙学畴壁芯部的标量凝聚体充当标准模型与暗扇区之间的混合门户，热浴费米子穿过畴壁时被部分、非热地转化为暗物质粒子。转化概率由壁轮廓决定的单一无量纲混合面积控制，产额由网络的面密度决定。轻暗粒子的转化概率与入射能量和暗质量无关，遗迹丰度塌缩为从keV Lyman-α下限到壁形成尺度的单一质量-耦合关系；重粒子情形下转化将单个入射费米子的能量转化为暗质量，可填充质量超过浴温度和介子质量两个尺度的暗扇区。该门户同时产生普通freeze-in，但DMC凭借相干壁面积增强在固定耦合下比freeze-in高1-2个数量级，且在freeze-in关闭的重粒子区间依然有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有暗物质产生机制（如freeze-in）在重暗粒子区间（质量超过浴温度和介子质量）失效，且缺乏高产的暗物质产生通道。作者旨在提出一种新的非热产生机制，利用宇宙学畴壁这一拓扑缺陷作为门户，实现从标准模型粒子到暗扇区的高效、非热转化，从而覆盖包括重暗物质在内的更广质量范围。

Method: 提出的DMC机制构建如下：宇宙学畴壁在核心处容纳标量凝聚体，该凝聚体作为标准模型与暗扇区之间的混合门户；热浴费米子穿越壁时发生部分非热转化。转化概率由壁剖面定义的无量纲混合面积决定，产额由畴壁网络的面积密度决定。作者通过解析计算分别处理轻暗粒子（转化概率与入射能量和暗质量无关）和重暗粒子（单费米子能量全部转化为暗质量）两种情形，并与标准freeze-in机制进行对比。

Result: 轻暗粒子情形下，遗迹丰度统一塌缩为从keV Lyman-α下限到壁形成尺度的单一质量-耦合关系；重暗粒子情形下可填充质量远超浴温度和介子质量的暗扇区。在固定耦合下，DMC因相干壁面积增强而比freeze-in高1-2个数量级，并且在freeze-in完全关闭的重粒子区间仍能保持有效运作。

Conclusion: DMC为暗物质产生提供了一种全新的非热机制，凭借畴壁的相干面积增强效应，在轻、重两个暗粒子质量区间均表现出优于传统freeze-in的产生效率，尤其弥补了freeze-in在重粒子区间的失效空白，为理解暗物质起源及暗扇区结构提供了新的理论路径。

Abstract: We propose a new mechanism for dark matter genesis, \emph{Defect-Mediated Conversion} (DMC). Cosmological domain walls can host a scalar condensate in their core acting as a mixing portal between the Standard Model and a dark sector: thermal-bath fermions crossing the wall are partially and non-thermally converted into dark particles. The conversion probability is governed by a single dimensionless mixing area built from the wall profile, and the yield by the area density of the network. For a light dark particle the probability is independent of both the incident energy and the dark mass, and the relic abundance collapses onto a single mass--coupling relation running from the keV Lyman-$α$ floor to the wall-formation scale. For a heavy one, conversion turns the energy of a single incident fermion into the dark mass, populating dark sectors with masses exceeding both the bath temperature and the mediator mass. The same portal also sources ordinary freeze-in, but DMC, boosted by the coherent wall-area enhancement, dominates it by one to two orders of magnitude at fixed couplings and remains operative in the heavy regime, where freeze-in shuts off.

</details>


### [111] [Singlet-doublet dark matter beyond freeze-out](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12480)
*Prudhvi N. Bhattiprolu,Aaron Pierce,Alexander Takla*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了Singlet-Doublet费米子暗物质模型，该模型通过电弱玻色子相互作用实现暗物质丰度，根据汤川耦合强度可通过冻结、共散射、冻结入或SuperWIMP机制产生暗物质。研究重点是小汤川耦合区域，在此极限下直接和间接探测困难，但具有丰富的对撞机现象学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨一个经济型的弱尺度暗物质模型——Singlet-Doublet费米子模型，系统分析其在不同汤川耦合参数下实现暗物质丰度的多种机制，特别关注小耦合格局下的可探测性前景，为暗物质实验研究提供理论指导。

Method: 系统分析Singlet-Doublet费米子暗物质模型的热历史，按汤川耦合大小分类讨论暗物质丰度产生机制（冻结/共湮灭、共散射、冻结入、SuperWIMP），并重点研究小汤川耦合极限下的对撞机现象学特征。

Result: 该模型可通过四种机制产生暗物质丰度：大汤川耦合时通过冻结（含共湮灭）机制，小耦合时通过共散射、冻结入或SuperWIMP机制。在小汤川耦合区域，直接和间接探测信号被强烈抑制，但对撞机探测前景良好，可能产生丰富的可观测信号。

Conclusion: Singlet-Doublet费米子模型在小汤川耦合极限下虽难以被传统的直接/间接探测手段观测，但对撞机实验有望成为探测该暗物质模型的关键途径，丰富了暗物质研究的实验策略。

Abstract: The Singlet-Doublet fermion model of dark matter is an economical weak-scale dark matter model that realizes the dark matter abundance through interactions with the electroweak bosons of the Standard Model. Depending on the size of the Yukawa couplings in the model, the dark matter relic abundance can be produced via freeze-out (including co-annihilation), co-scattering, freeze-in, or the SuperWIMP mechanism. We analyze the ways this model can realize the dark matter density with emphasis on the small Yukawa coupling regime. In this limit, direct and indirect detection are difficult, but a rich collider phenomenology is possible.

</details>


### [112] [Singlet-Doublet fermion origin of dark matter, neutrino mass and inverse first-order electroweak phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12483)
*Debasish Borah,Indrajit Saha,Sujit Kumar Sahoo,Narendra Sahu,Shashwat Sharma*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在一种scotogenic型辐射中微子质量模型中，通过引入singlet-doublet (SD)费米子作为暗物质候选者，实现逆一阶电弱相变（IFOEWPT）并产生可观测引力波信号的可能性。模型扩展了标准模型，包含两代SU(2)_L单重态和双重态费米子、一个标量单重态以及三个右手中微子，在正确产生中微子质量、暗物质丰度和IFOEWPT的前提下约束参数空间，并指出该模型可在未来的对撞机、直接探测、引力波和CMB实验中检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索scotogenic型模型中同时实现逆一阶电弱相变和可观测引力波信号的可能性，将中微子质量产生、暗物质候选者与宇宙学相变物理联系起来，寻找可以在多个实验渠道中被检验的新物理模型。

Method: 构建一个扩展标准模型的scotogenic型辐射中微子质量模型：引入两代SU(2)_L单重态和双重态费米子（产生一圆圈图的中微子质量）、一个标量单重态以及三个右手中微子。其中较轻的SD费米子中性分量在残余Z_2对称性下稳定，作为暗物质候选者；较重的SD费米子与Higgs强耦合，驱动宇宙从对称相到最终破缺相经历两次一级相变（IFOEWPT）。通过中微子质量、暗物质遗迹密度和IFOEWPT等要求结合实验约束来限制参数空间。

Result: 获得了满足中微子质量产生、暗物质遗迹密度和IFOEWPT要求的同时兼容现有实验约束的参数空间；该允许的参数空间可在未来的对撞机、暗物质直接探测、引力波和CMB实验中探测。

Conclusion: 该scotogenic型SD费米子模型能够同时产生逆一阶电弱相变和相应的引力波信号，为解释中微子质量、暗物质与宇宙学相变提供统一的框架，且模型的参数空间具有多实验渠道的可检验性。

Abstract: We study the possibility of an inverse first-order electroweak phase transition (IFOEWPT) and observable gravitational waves (GW) in a radiative neutrino mass model of scotogenic type where singlet-doublet (SD) fermions, the lightest of whom is the dark matter (DM) candidate, generate the necessary seesaw at one-loop level. Considering the possibility of light neutrinos being Dirac for simplicity and additional detection prospects, we extend the standard model (SM) with two generations of $SU(2)_L$ singlet and doublet fermions, one singlet scalar, and three right-handed neutrinos (RHNs). While RHNs provide the right chiral parts of light Dirac neutrinos, the SD fermions and the scalar singlet facilitate the one-loop neutrino mass diagram. The neutral component of the lighter SD fermion, stabilized under a residual $Z_2$ symmetry plays the role of DM while the heavier SD fermions strongly couple to the Higgs leading to an IFOEWPT where the Universe undergoes two different first-order phase transition as it goes from the symmetric to the final broken Higgs phase. We constrain the parameter space from the requirements of generating the correct neutrino mass, DM relic as well as IFOEWPT while incorporating the existing constraints from different experiments. The final allowed parameter space of the model can be probed at collider, direct-detection, GW and cosmic microwave background (CMB) experiments in near future.

</details>


### [113] [Spontaneous Scoto-leptogenesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12497)
*Arghyajit Datta,Hyun Min Lee,Jun-Ho Song*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种低能标自发轻子生成机制（自发性Scotogenic轻子生成），在动力学最小Scotogenic模型中同时解释中微子质量、惰性标量暗物质和宇宙重子不对称性。通过全局U(1)_{B-L}对称性破缺产生的滚动Majoron诱导有效化学势，将最轻右手中微子的可行质量标度降低到TeV范围，并探讨了多组分暗物质（惰性标量+Majoron）的实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有热Scotogenic轻子生成机制要求右手中微子具有较高的质量标度，难以被直接实验检验。作者希望构建一个低能标框架，能够同时统一解释中微子质量、暗物质和宇宙重子不对称性三个基本问题，并使相关的粒子（特别是最轻右手中微子）在TeV尺度上可被实验直接测试。

Method: 在最小动力学Scotogenic模型中引入全局U(1)_{B-L}对称性的自发破缺，产生滚动的Majoron场。滚动Majoron在存在B-L破坏相互作用（右手中微子的衰变和逆衰变）的情况下为B-L荷提供有效化学势，通过电弱Sphaleron过程转化为观测到的重子不对称性。该机制在强冲刷（strong washout）区域有效，并分析了λ_5耦合在自发轻子生成和惰性标量暗物质（通过擦除惰性标量不对称性）中的作用，同时探讨了通过动力学失配（kinetic misalignment）机制产生Majoron暗物质的方案。

Result: 该机制成功将最轻右手中微子的质量标度降低到TeV范围，使具有两个层级右手中微子的热Scotogenic轻子生成可通过直接实验测试。实现了一个包含惰性标量和Majoron的多组分暗物质扇区，整个框架可通过直接探测实验、对撞机惰性标量搜索以及未来Majoron暗物质或暗辐射探测等多种互补实验途径进行检验。

Conclusion: 该框架为低能标重子生成、中微子质量和多组分暗物质扇区提供了统一起源，建立了一个可由直接探测实验、对撞机搜索和未来暗物质或暗辐射探测等互补实验手段联合检验的综合性理论模型。

Abstract: We propose a low-scale spontaneous leptogenesis scenario within the dynamical minimal scotogenic model for accommodating neutrino masses and inert scalar dark matter simultaneously. Thus, we dub the mechanism Spontaneous Scoto-leptogenesis. In this setup, a rolling Majoron arising from the global $U(1)_{B-L}$ symmetry breaking induces an effective chemical potential for the $B-L$ charge in the presence of $B-L$ violating interactions that allow for the efficient decays and inverse decays of right handed neutrinos (RHN), so it gives rise to the observed baryon asymmetry of the Universe through the electroweak sphaleron conversion. The mechanism becomes effective in the strong washout regime and successfully lowers the viable mass scale of the lightest RHN to the range of TeV scales, thereby making the thermal scotogenic leptogenesis with two hierarchical RHNs accessible to direct tests. We identify the roles of the $λ_5$ coupling for spontaneous leptogenesis and inert scalar dark matter through the efficient erasure of the inert scalar asymmetry. We also explore the regime for Majoron dark matter from the kinetic misalignment, showing that a multicomponent dark sector comprising the inert scalar and the Majoron can be realized in the model. The resulting framework provides a unified origin for low-scale baryogenesis, neutrino masses, and multicomponent dark sector, so it can be tested by complementary experimental probes through direct detection experiments, collider searches for inert scalars, and future detection of Majoron dark matter or dark radiation.

</details>


### [114] [N$^3$LO soft-gluon corrections for $Zγ$ production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12556)
*Nikolaos Kidonakis,Kaan Şimşek*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了Zγ产生的N^3LO阶软胶子修正，并利用1PI运动学结果给出LHC质子-质子碰撞中Zγ产生截面的近似N^3LO（aN^3LO）QCD预测。在13 TeV和13.6 TeV对撞能量下计算了光子横动量p_T^γ和快度y^γ的微分截面，系统检查了高阶K因子，量化了各微扰阶的标度与PDF不确定性，发现高p_T区域软胶子修正显著且aN^3LO具有良好的微扰收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zγ产生过程在LHC的精确测量是检验标准模型和搜索新物理的重要探针，需要更精确的高阶QCD理论预言来匹配实验精度，尤其是高p_T区域的软胶子修正效应。

Method: 在1PI运动学框架下计算Zγ产生的软胶子修正至N^3LO阶，利用该结果构建近似N^3LO（aN^3LO）QCD预测，计算13 TeV和13.6 TeV能量下光子横动量与快度的微分截面，并系统分析K因子、标度不确定性和PDF不确定性。

Result: 软胶子修正在高p_T区域显著，aN^3LO阶展现出良好的微扰收敛性；获得了精确的微分截面预测并量化了各阶的标度与PDF不确定度。

Conclusion: aN^3LO预测提供了Zγ产生过程目前最精确的理论计算之一，有助于LHC实验数据进行更严格的理论-实验对比，并可用于新物理搜索。

Abstract: We calculate soft-gluon corrections for $Zγ$ production through next-to-next-to-next-to-leading-order (N$^3$LO) in one-particle-inclusive (1PI) kinematics. We use these results to produce approximate N$^3$LO (aN$^3$LO) QCD predictions for $Zγ$ production cross sections in proton-proton collisions at LHC energies. Differential cross sections in photon transverse momentum $p_T^γ$ and rapidity $y^γ$ are computed at 13 TeV and 13.6 TeV collider energies. Higher-order $K$-factors are examined systematically, and scale and PDF uncertainties are quantified at each perturbative order. Soft-gluon corrections are found to be significant in the high-$p_T$ region, with good perturbative convergence observed at aN$^3$LO.

</details>


### [115] [A Novel One-loop Model for Majorana Neutrino Mass and Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12646)
*Mohamed Belfkir,Mohamed Amin Loualidi,Salah Nasri*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次实现了Majorana中微子质量的有限单圈T4-3-i拓扑的完整场论构造。通过引入Dirac费米子N作为介体，将轻子数破坏置于圈内独立的Majorana费米子ψ中，并施加Z2对称性消除低阶seesaw贡献，给出了最小singlet-doublet实现（T4-3-i-B1），并全面检验了该模型与中微子振荡、宇宙学、轻子味破坏、电弱精确观测量、暗物质等多方面的相容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T4-3-i是Weinberg算符的单圈实现拓扑，其中费米子连接两个外部轻子-Higgs对。若该费米子为Majorana singlet或triplet，则同一相互作用会生成tree-level的I型或III型seesaw贡献，使圈图不再是中微子质量的主导来源。为消除这一低阶贡献，需要将介体取为Dirac费米子，使该拓扑成为中微子质量的唯一来源。

Method: 作者对T4-3-i拓扑的允许电弱荷赋值进行分类，选取Dirac费米子N作为连接外腿的介体，将轻子数破坏放在圈内独立的Majorana费米子ψ中，并施加精确的Z2对称性使新标量保持惰性并稳定最轻奇数态。在此基础上构造了最小singlet-doublet实现T4-3-i-B1，包含一个Dirac费米子、一个Majorana费米子、一个惰性标量二重态和一个惰性标量单态。

Result: 所得中微子质量矩阵秩为2，预言一个无质量中微子。模型同时适用于正序和反序中微子质量排序，并满足中微子振荡、宇宙学数据、带电轻子味破坏（含μ-e转化）、电弱精密观测量、h→γγ、理论一致性条件、遗迹丰度及直接探测限等全部约束。费米子和标量暗物质候选均可行：费米子候选仅通过圈诱导的Higgs耦合，自旋无关散射率被强烈压低；标量候选通过tree-level Higgs门户耦合，可高于中微子地板同时与当前限制相容。两种情况下，与惰性标量的共湮灭对再现观测遗迹丰度至关重要。

Conclusion: T4-3-i-B1是首个完整且自洽的T4-3-i拓扑实现，为中微子质量和暗物质提供一个统一的、可被全面实验检验的理论框架。该模型同时预言一个无质量中微子，并给出费米子与标量两种暗物质方案，二者在当前实验约束下均可行。

Abstract: We present the first complete field-theoretic realization of the finite one-loop T4-3-i topology for Majorana neutrino mass. Here, T4-3-i denotes a one-loop realization of the Weinberg operator in which a fermion links the two external lepton--Higgs pairs. If this fermion is a Majorana singlet or triplet, the same interactions generate a tree-level type-I or type-III seesaw contribution, respectively, so that the loop is not the leading source of neutrino mass. This lower-order contribution is removed by taking the mediator to be a Dirac fermion $N$, placing lepton-number violation in a separate Majorana fermion $ψ$ inside the loop, and imposing an exact $Z_2$ symmetry that keeps the new scalars inert and stabilizes the lightest odd state. We classify the allowed electroweak charge assignments and study the minimal singlet-doublet realization, denoted T4-3-i-B1, which contains one Dirac fermion, one Majorana fermion, an inert scalar doublet, and an inert scalar singlet. The resulting rank-two neutrino mass matrix predicts one massless neutrino. We confront both normal and inverted neutrino-mass orderings with neutrino-oscillation and cosmological data, charged-lepton flavor violation, including $μ-e$ conversion, electroweak precision observables, $h\toγγ$, theoretical consistency conditions, the relic abundance, and direct-detection limits. Both fermionic and scalar dark matter are viable. The fermionic candidate has only a loop-induced Higgs coupling and consequently a strongly suppressed spin-independent scattering rate, whereas the scalar candidate couples through a tree-level Higgs portal and can lie above the neutrino floor while remaining compatible with current limits. In both cases, coannihilation with inert scalars is essential for reproducing the observed relic abundance.

</details>


### [116] [Massive cold hybrid stars in a modified Polyakov-Nambu-Jona-Lasinio model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12653)
*Sk Md Adil Imam,Pedro Costa,Mariana Dutra,Odilon Lourenço,Renan Pereira,Constança Providência*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种修正的Polyakov-loop Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (mPNJL)模型，其中Polyakov势显式依赖夸克化学势，使其在零温下仍保持有限，从而描述冷稠密物质中的禁闭-退禁闭相变。通过Maxwell结构与强子态方程结合，发现该体系可呈现一次或两次相变，并生成含夸克物质核心的稳定混合星，其最大质量可超过2倍太阳质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PNJL模型在零温下的Polyakov势会发散或失效，无法用于描述冷稠密夸克物质中的禁闭-退禁闭转变。作者希望构建一个在零温下仍有效的模型，以系统研究冷致密星内部可能存在的夸克物质相及其相结构。

Method: （1）在PNJL模型中引入依赖夸克化学势的修正Polyakov势，构造mPNJL模型。（2）通过Maxwell构造将该修正夸克扇区与强子态方程（EoS）连接。（3）系统扫描Polyakov势参数与夸克矢量相互作用强度，分析相变位置、态方程刚度及混合星性质（质量-半径关系、声速等）。

Result: （1）依据参数选择，EoS可呈现两次相变（强子态→禁闭夸克/“quarkyonic”态→退禁闭夸克态）或单次直接跃迁。（2）排斥矢量相互作用是获得稳定夸克核心的必要条件。（3）在低密度区采用足够刚硬的强子EoS时，含quarkyonic和/或退禁闭核心的混合星最大质量可超过2 M_sun。（4）最大质量构型的核心中，quarkyonic星声速超过共形极限c_s^2=1/3。

Conclusion: 该工作系统阐明了各模型参数在塑造混合星结构中的定性作用，为理解冷稠密QCD物质相结构及大质量中子星内部可能存在多相夸克核心提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We propose a modified Polyakov-loop Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (mPNJL) model in which the Polyakov potential is given by an explicit dependence on the quark chemical potential, allowing it to remain finite at zero temperature and thus to describe the confinement-deconfinement transition in cold dense matter. Combining this modified quark sector with hadronic equations of state via a Maxwell construction, we find that, depending on the model parameters, the equation of state can exhibit either two phase transitions, from hadronic matter to confined (quarkyonic) quark matter and subsequently to deconfined quark matter, or a single transition directly from hadronic to deconfined quark matter or from hadronic to quarkyonic quark matter. Stable massive cold hybrid stars with only quarkyonic and/or deconfined quark phase are obtained. We systematically examine how the parameters of the modified Polyakov potential and the quark vector interactions control the location of these transitions, and find that repulsive vector interactions are essential to obtain a stable quark core. Hybrid stars with quarkyonic and/or a deconfined core can reach maximum masses above $2M_\odot$, provided a sufficiently stiff hadronic equation of state is used at low density. In the core of the maximum-mass configurations, the speed of sound exceeds the conformal limit, $c_s^2 = 1/3$, for the quarkyonic core stars. This work establishes the qualitative role of each model parameter in shaping hybrid-star structure.

</details>


### [117] [Scalar induced gravitational waves as probes of dark QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12706)
*Wan-Zhe Feng,Ao Li,Jing-Zhi Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量诱导引力波（SIGWs）作为探测暗QCD交叉相变的手段，构建了包含SM和暗QCD扇区的有效能量/熵自由度模型，通过求解一阶标量扰动和二阶SIGW，证明了暗QCD交叉相变会在SIGW谱中产生频率偏移的畸变特征，可作为隐藏约束扇区的特征信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型QCD交叉相变会在标量诱导引力波谱中留下印记，而现有的SIGW研究多采用理想化的辐射主导宇宙热历史。受孪生希格斯和不对称孪生重子暗物质模型的启发，本文提出利用SIGWs探测具有更高约束能标的暗QCD扇区，作为一种补充性的宇宙学探针来揭示隐藏的约束扇区。

Method: 1) 构建SM加上暗QCD扇区（含三种轻暗夸克或全部六种暗夸克）的有效能量和熵自由度；2) 计算相应的物态方程参数和声速；3) 利用这些热力学量求解一阶标量扰动和二阶SIGWs；4) 采用单色原初曲率功率谱，对比理想辐射主导与真实SM热历史的结果，并分析暗QCD交叉对SIGW谱的调制效应。

Result: 研究证明了：1) 真实SM热历史相对于理想化辐射主导情形会显著修改SIGW谱；2) 暗QCD交叉相变在增强标量模重新进入地平线（接近暗约束能标）时会额外产生频率移位的畸变特征；3) 该特征相对于SM QCD印记移向更高频率，可作为暗QCD扇区的独特签名。

Conclusion: SIGWs为隐藏约束扇区提供了互补性的宇宙学探测手段，其频率移位的畸变特征可作为暗QCD扇区的识别信号。该分析方法可推广到其他包含不同暗约束扇区的理论模型。

Abstract: We investigate scalar induced gravitational waves (SIGWs) as probes of a dark QCD crossover. Motivated by twin Higgs and asymmetric twin baryon dark matter scenarios, we consider a dark QCD sector with a confinement scale approximately 5.5 times the Standard Model (SM) QCD scale. We construct the effective energy and entropy degrees of freedom for the SM supplemented by dark QCD sectors containing either three light dark quark flavors or all six dark quark flavors. The resulting equation of state parameter and sound speed are then used to solve the first-order scalar perturbations and the second-order SIGWs through the SM and dark QCD crossover epochs. For a monochromatic primordial curvature power spectrum, we first demonstrate that the realistic SM thermal history modifies the SIGW spectrum relative to the idealized radiation-dominated case. We then show that a dark QCD crossover generates an additional frequency-shifted distortion when the enhanced scalar mode reenters the horizon near the dark confinement scale. This distinctive feature can therefore serve as a characteristic signature of the dark QCD sector. Our results demonstrate that SIGWs provide a complementary cosmological probe of hidden confining sectors, with characteristic spectral features shifted to higher frequencies relative to the SM QCD imprint. The analysis developed in this work can also be extended to other well-motivated theories containing different dark confining sectors.

</details>


### [118] [Topological diagrams of $Ω^0_c$ decays in the $SU(3)_F$ limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12742)
*Ying-Xin Lai,Di Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在SU(3)味对称性极限下系统研究了Ω_c^0重子通过弱衰变到十重态和八重态重子的拓扑振幅，推导了拓扑振幅与SU(3)不可约振幅之间的线性关系，并获得了若干同位旋关系及用于检验Körner-Pati-Woo定理的关系式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ω_c^0是独特的粲六重态重子，只能通过弱相互作用衰变，而其衰变振幅的结构尚未被完整理解。研究其拓扑振幅有助于深入理解粲重子的弱衰变机制。

Method: 在SU(3)_F对称性极限下，系统列出Ω_c^0衰变到十重态和八重态重子的树图和企鹅图贡献的拓扑振幅。通过张量分析方法，推导拓扑振幅与SU(3)不可约振幅之间的线性对应关系。

Result: 完整给出了Ω_c^0衰变到十重态和八重态重子的树图和企鹅图拓扑振幅；推导了拓扑振幅与SU(3)不可约振幅的线性关系；获得了若干同位旋关系，并导出了用于检验Körner-Pati-Woo定理的关系式。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解和检验Ω_c^0弱衰变的振幅结构提供了完整的理论框架，所推导的同位旋关系和相关定理检验关系可用于未来实验数据的验证。

Abstract: The $Ω^0_c$ baryon is a unique charmed sextet baryon as it decays through weak interaction. In this work, we investigate the topological amplitudes of $Ω_c^0$ decays in the $SU(3)_F$ limit. The tree- and penguin-induced diagrams contributing to $Ω_c^0$ decays into decuplet and octet baryons are presented completely. The linear relations between the topological amplitudes and the $SU(3)$ irreducible amplitudes are derived via tensor analysis. Several isospin relations are obtained, and some relations are derived to test the Körner-Pati-Woo theorem.

</details>


### [119] [In-medium properties of $D$ and $D^*$ mesons in magnetized isospin asymmetric nuclear matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12834)
*Saksham Sharma,Dhananjay Singh,Suneel Dutt,Harleen Dahiya,Arvind Kumar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了外磁场对有限温度、同位旋不对称核物质中赝标量（D^0, D^+, D_s）和矢量（D^{0*}, D^{+*}, D_s^*）介子介质性质的影响，采用手征SU(3)夸克平均场（CQMF）模型与光锥夸克模型（LFQM）的混合理论框架，发现磁场通过磁催化增强有效质量和衰变常数，而重子密度增大导致吸引质量移动并抑制衰变常数和分布振幅，朗道能级与赝标量-矢量混合效应分别产生不同方向的质量修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强相互作用物质中重味介子的介质性质对探索量子色动力学（QCD）相图具有重要意义，而外部磁场（如非对心重离子碰撞中产生的强磁场）与有限温度、有限重子密度及同位旋不对称等条件下的重味介子行为尚未被系统研究，亟需建立理论框架以揭示磁场与密度效应的竞争机制。

Method: 采用混合理论框架：首先通过包含磁化Dirac海贡献和核子反常磁矩的CQMF模型计算介质修正的组分夸克质量；然后将其作为LFQM的输入，计算介子质量、弱衰变常数和前导扭度分布振幅；对带电介子限制在最低朗道能级的朗道量子化效应进行了处理，并对每个D-D*二重态考虑了磁场诱导的赝标量-矢量混合。

Result: 外磁场通过磁催化效应增强赝标量和矢量D介子的有效质量与衰变常数；重子密度增大倾向于产生吸引力质量移动并抑制衰变常数和分布振幅；朗道能级贡献进一步增强了带电介子的有效质量；赝标量-矢量混合产生能级排斥，使矢量介子质量上移、赝标量介子质量下移；同位旋不对称在所有介子态中诱导了小而系统的质量劈裂。

Conclusion: 磁场与密度效应的相互作用产生非平凡的介质行为，揭示了重轻介子性质对磁场、密度和同位旋不对称的复杂依赖关系，为FAIR、NICA和J-PARC等设施上的重味动力学研究提供了理论参考和预言。

Abstract: We investigate the impact of an external magnetic field on the in-medium properties of pseudoscalar ($D^0,D^+,D_s$) and vector ($D^{0*},D^{+*},D_{s}^{*}$) mesons in isospin asymmetric nuclear matter at finite temperature using a hybrid theoretical framework combining the chiral SU(3) quark mean-field (CQMF) model and the light-front quark model (LFQM). The medium-modified constituent quark masses, obtained from the CQMF model by including the magnetized Dirac sea contribution and anomalous magnetic moments of nucleons, are used as input to the LFQM calculations of meson masses, weak decay constants, and leading-twist distribution amplitudes. We further incorporate the Landau quantization of the charged mesons restricted to the lowest Landau level, while magnetic field induced pseudoscalar-vector mixing is taken into account for each $D$-$D^{*}$ doublet. We find that the external magnetic field enhances the effective masses and decay constants of both pseudoscalar and vector $D$ mesons by magnetic catalysis, while increasing baryon density generally induces an attractive mass shift and suppresses the decay constant and distribution amplitudes. The Landau level contribution further enhances the effective masses of the charged mesons, whereas the pseudoscalar-vector mixing produces a level repulsion, shifting the vector meson masses upward and the pseudoscalar meson masses downward. The interplay between magnetic field and density effects gives rise to a nontrivial medium behavior of heavy-light meson properties, with isospin asymmetry further inducing a small but systematic mass splitting across all the meson states considered. These results provide useful insights into heavy-flavor dynamics in strongly interacting matter and are relevant to ongoing and future studies at FAIR, NICA, and J-PARC.

</details>


### [120] [Entropy from inclusive scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12923)
*M. A. Braun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在无坡密子间相互作用的幺正坡密子交换模型中，从多个非弹性截面引入概率的问题。研究发现由此产生的非弹性截面与幺正性要求的结果存在差异，但该差异随能量升高而减小，并在高能极限下消失。数值计算了概率和熵，结果表明熵随能量先上升，在快度12-17处达到最大值后随更高能量缓慢下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在坡密子交换模型中引入概率的方法是否与幺正性条件自洽，并考察由此导出的熵随能量的变化规律，以验证或修正对高能散射过程中熵行为的"朴素预期"。

Method: 在无坡密子间相互作用的幺正坡密子交换模型中，从多个非弹性截面构建概率分布，与幺正性给出的非弹性截面进行比较；对概率和熵进行数值计算。

Result: 观测到从多截面引入的概率所得非弹性截面与幺正性结果存在差异，该差异随能量增加而减小并在高能极限消失。熵随能量先增加，在快度约12-17处达到峰值后随能量升高缓慢下降，与朴素预期不同。

Conclusion: 在该模型框架下，多截面概率引入方法在高能极限下与幺正性自洽，但有限能量下存在偏差；熵的非单调行为表明高能散射中的熵不一定是单调递增的。

Abstract: Introduction of probabilities from multiple inclusive cross-sections is studied
  in the unitary pomeron exchange models with no interaction between the pomerons themselves.
  Discrepancy is observed between the emerging inelasic cross-section and the one following from unitarity.
  It diminishes with energy and disappears in the high-energy limit. Probabilities and
  entropy are calculated numerically. Contrary to naive predictions as a function of energy the entropy first rises and after achievung its maximum at rapidity 12-17 slowly falls for higher energies

</details>


### [121] [Newtrinos.jl: A Julia Package for Global Analysis of Neutrino Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12940)
*Philipp Eller,David Schultheiß*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍Newtrinos.jl，一个开源Julia包，用于中微子数据全局分析。它采用模块化三层架构（物理模型、实验描述、统计推断分离），支持Frequentist和Bayesian推断，可并行计算，全自动微分，并支持实验间参数的相关/去相关处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子数据全局分析需要灵活组合多种实验和理论模型，现有工具缺乏模块化和可组合性，难以快速集成新实验和物理模型，且对现代统计推断工作流（如系统不确定性建模、联合似然构建）支持不足。

Method: 采用模块化三层架构，将物理模型、实验描述和统计推断分离为独立模块；为每个实验数据集实现完整的统计前向模型（包含所有相关系统不确定性），同时定义似然函数和数据生成过程；支持在实验间合并、相关或去相关物理参数和扰动物理参数；基于Julia实现全自动微分和CPU线程/分布式并行化。

Result: 实现了可组合的框架，能轻松构建多数据集的联合似然；无需修改核心代码即可添加新实验和物理模型；同时支持Frequentist和Bayesian推断，支持精确梯度计算与并行化。

Conclusion: Newtrinos.jl为中微子分析提供了一个灵活、可扩展且统计严谨的开源工具，通过架构模块化和自动微分能力，提高了全局拟合的效率与可接受性。

Abstract: Newtrinos.jl is an open-source Julia package for performing global analyses of neutrino data. It provides a modular, three-layer architecture that separates physics models, experiment descriptions, and statistical inference into independent modules. This allows researchers to freely combine experiments and test them against a variety of theoretical models. New experiments and physics models can be added without modifying core code. Full statistical forward models including all relevant systematic uncertainties are implemented for each experimental dataset, defining both the likelihood and the data-generating process and enabling modern statistical inference workflows. The framework is composable, making it straightforward to construct joint likelihoods over multiple datasets. Physics and nuisance parameters can be merged, correlated, or decorrelated across experiments to ensure consistency in the joint fit. The package supports Frequentist and Bayesian inference and is parallelizable across CPU threads or distributed workers. Written entirely in Julia, all models are automatically differentiable, enabling exact gradient computation.

</details>


### [122] [Decay widths of radially excited vector mesons of the $U(3)$ nonet with production of three pseudoscalar mesons in the extended NJL model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13140)
*M. K. Volkov,A. A. Pivovarov,K. Nurlan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在扩展的NJL模型中，计算了第一径向激发矢量介子ρ'、ω'、φ'和K*'衰变产生三个赝标介子（K, π, η）的过程，考虑了盒形图通道和含基态及第一径向激发态中间矢量介子的通道，结果可作为未来实验的预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究第一径向激发矢量介子衰变到三个赝标介子的过程，为未来实验提供理论预言，填补该衰变模式在理论上尚未系统研究的空白。

Method: 采用扩展的NJL（Nambu-Jona-Lasinio）有效场论模型，计算ρ'、ω'、φ'和K*'衰变到三个赝标介子（P=K,π,η）的衰变振幅。计算中考虑了盒形（box）图通道，以及含基态和第一径向激发态中间矢量介子的通道。

Result: 给出了第一径向激发矢量介子衰变到三个赝标介子的计算结果，作为对这些衰变过程的理论预言，可供未来实验检验。

Conclusion: 扩展NJL模型能够对第一径向激发矢量介子衰变到三赝标介子的过程给出理论预言，这些结果有助于未来实验对这些衰变模式的探测与验证。

Abstract: In the extended NJL model, the decays of the first radially excited vector mesons $ρ'$,$ω'$, $φ'$ and ${K^*}'$ with production of $3P$, where $P=K,π,η$ are calculated. The box channels and the channels with intermediate vector mesons in the ground and first radially excited states are considered. The obtained results can be considered as predictions for future experiments.

</details>


### [123] [Magnetization and Magnetic Field-Induced Correction: Implications for QGP Thermal Photon Production in Magnetohydrodynamic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13364)
*Jing Jing,Duan She,Ze-Fang Jiang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在(1+1)维相对论磁流体力学框架下系统研究了磁化夸克-胶子等离子体（QGP）的热光子发射，同时纳入磁化率χ_m（包括常数和基于格点QCD的温度依赖参数化）以及弱场量子修正f_EM。结果表明光子产额主要由初始磁场强度和其随时间衰减的轮廓主导，χ_m影响可忽略，而弱场修正f_EM在中横动量区域会产生显著增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强磁场下QGP的电磁辐射对于探测早期重离子碰撞中产生的超强磁场至关重要，需要在相对论磁流体力学框架中系统量化磁效应（磁化率和弱场量子修正）对热光子产额的影响。

Method: 采用Pu-Bjorken MHD框架，计算康普顿散射、qq湮灭、轫致辐射和重散射湮灭等过程的光子产生率，并沿QGP时空演化积分得到横动量(p_T)谱。系统比较了常数与格点QCD温度依赖的磁化率参数化，以及弱场量子修正对费米子分布函数的影响。

Result: 光子产额主要由初始磁场强度和其时间衰减轮廓主导；磁化率χ_m在所探索的参数空间中对光子产额影响可忽略；弱场修正f_EM在中横动量区域对热光子产生具有明显增强效应。

Conclusion: 该工作为磁化QGP中电磁观测量建立了严格的理论框架，并为未来纳入自旋-磁化动力学的耗散磁流体力学研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We investigate thermal photon emission from magnetized quark-gluon plasma (QGP) within (1+1)-dimensional relativistic magnetohydrodynamics (MHD), systematically incorporating magnetic susceptibility $χ_m$---encompassing both constant and lattice-QCD-derived temperature-dependent $χ_m(T)$ parametrizations---and weak-field quantum corrections to quark distribution functions $f_{\rm EM}$. Employing the Pu-Bjorken MHD framework, we calculate photon production rates from Compton scattering, $q\bar{q}$ annihilation, bremsstrahlung, and annihilation with rescattering, and integrate these over the QGP spacetime evolution to obtain transverse momentum ($p_T$) spectra. Our results demonstrate that photon yields are predominantly governed by the initial magnetic field strength and its temporal decay profile, with $χ_m$ exerting negligible influence in the explored parameter space. In contrast, the weak-field correction $f_{\rm EM}$ induces a distinct enhancement in thermal photon production at intermediate $p_T$. This work establishes a rigorous theoretical framework for quantifying electromagnetic observables in magnetized QGP and provides the foundation for future dissipative MHD studies incorporating spin-magnetization dynamics.

</details>


### [124] [Non-perturbative quark production and transport in the evolving glasma: the WAGASHI event generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13393)
*Nicholas J. Benoit,Chiho Nonaka,Hidetoshi Taya*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个新的模型WAGASHI，将经典Yang-Mills glasma演化、Wong方程输运和Schwinger对产生结合在逐事件的基础上，研究相对论重离子碰撞早期glasma阶段的非微扰夸克-反夸克对产生及夸克动力学。研究发现大量夸克在glasma阶段就已产生，其数量可与最终强子产额相比，且经历了显著的动量展宽和自旋随机化，暗示对夸克胶子等离子体早期热化有重要贡献。同时给出了Pb-Pb和O-O碰撞中重子数、电荷、奇异性及自旋极化的逐事件分布，发现较小O-O系统中存在特别大的涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解夸克胶子等离子体在最早期的热化和初始条件，需要在glasma阶段就纳入夸克自由度及其非微扰产生过程，以提供更真实的动力学初始条件用于后续流体动力学演化。

Method: 开发了一个名为WAGASHI的新模型，将三个关键组分相结合：(1) 经典Yang-Mills方程描述glasma演化；(2) Wong方程描述夸克在背景场中的输运；(3) Schwinger机制描述非微扰夸克-反夸克对产生。整个模拟以逐事件（event-by-event）为基础进行，能够给出各种守恒量分布。

Result: (1) glasma阶段产生的夸克数量可观，与最终强子产额可比；(2) 这些夸克经历显著的动量展宽和自旋随机化，表明对早期热化有实质贡献；(3) 获得了Pb-Pb和O-O碰撞中重子数、电荷、奇异性和自旋极化的逐事件分布，O-O小系统中涨落尤为显著。

Conclusion: 该模型为QGP后续流体动力学演化提供了动态的初始条件，且表明早期glasma阶段的夸克产生与输运对QGP的平衡化和最终观测量的涨落结构具有重要影响，尤其在较小的碰撞系统中。

Abstract: We study non-perturbative quark-antiquark pair production and the subsequent quark dynamics in the earliest glasma stage of relativistic heavy-ion collisions. For that, we develop a new model, WAGASHI (Wong-precessing Anisotropic Glasma And ScHwinger-produced Initial-conditions), which combines classical Yang-Mills glasma evolution, Wong-equation transport, and Schwinger pair production on an event-by-event basis. We find that a sizeable number of quarks, comparable to the final hadron yields, are produced already during the glasma stage and subsequently undergo substantial momentum broadening and spin randomization, suggesting a significant contribution toward the early equilibration of the quark-gluon plasma. We also determine the event-by-event distributions of baryon number, electric charge, strangeness, and spin polarization in Pb-Pb and O-O collisions at LHC energies, finding particularly large fluctuations in the smaller O-O system. These results provide dynamical initial conditions for the subsequent hydrodynamic evolution of the quark-gluon plasma.

</details>


### [125] [Critical behavior and critical exponents of rotating QCD matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13469)
*Kai Xiao,Fei Sun,Shuang Li,Xun Chen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在平均场近似下，利用两味Nambu--Jona-Lasinio（NJL）模型研究旋转强相互作用物质的热力学性质与临界行为，在温度-角速度（T,ω）平面上确定了相结构和临界端点（CEP），并提取了比热密度、旋转极化不连续性、旋转磁化率等热力学量的有效临界指数。所得指数趋近平均场预期值并满足标度关系，表明旋转自由度不改变平均场临界标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旋转效应对强相互作用物质（如重离子碰撞中产生的夸克胶子等离子体）相变及临界现象的影响，系统刻画旋转诱导的临界行为，为超越平均场近似的旋转QCD物质研究奠定基础。

Method: 采用两味NJL模型在平均场近似下进行研究；通过分析热力学量在临界端点附近的奇异性行为，提取比热密度、旋转极化不连续性、旋转磁化率以及旋转极化临界等温线等物理量的有效临界指数，并检验其满足的标度关系。

Result: 获得的临界指数趋近于平均场理论预期的标准值，且满足相应的标度关系，表明旋转自由度在本框架下不改变潜在的均场临界标度行为。

Conclusion: 该研究系统地表征了旋转诱导的临界现象，确认在平均场水平上旋转不改变临界标度行为，并为未来在超越平均场近似下研究旋转QCD物质提供了基础。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic properties and critical behavior of rotating strongly interacting matter within the two-flavor Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (NJL) model in the mean-field approximation. The phase structure and the critical endpoint (CEP) are determined in the temperature--angular velocity \((T,ω)\) plane. By analyzing the singular behavior of thermodynamic observables near the CEP, we extract the corresponding effective critical exponents characterizing the scaling behavior of the specific heat density, the rotational polarization discontinuity, the rotational susceptibility, and the critical-isotherm behavior of the rotational polarization. The obtained exponents approach the expected mean-field values and satisfy the corresponding scaling relations, indicating that the rotational degree of freedom does not alter the underlying mean-field critical scaling behavior within the present framework. These results provide a systematic characterization of rotation-induced critical phenomena and establish a basis for further studies of rotating QCD matter beyond the mean-field approximation.

</details>


### [126] [A pion-driven near-threshold enhancement in the $π\,T_{cc}$ system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13525)
*Pedro Brandão,Jorgivan Morais Dias,Luciano M. Abreu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究π介子散射于由DD*构成的强子分子态T_cc(3875)的三体动力学，在Fixed Center Approximation框架下求解Faddeev方程，发现πT_cc阈值附近存在约4055 MeV的共振增强结构，表明强子分子态可作为更复杂奇特强子的构建单元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究已实验确认的强子分子态能否作为更复杂奇特强子的基本构建单元，即验证轻介子与分子态的散射能否产生新的近阈值三体共振结构，扩展对奇特强子谱的理解。

Method: 将T_cc视为同位旋标量的DD*分子团簇，在I(J^P)=1(1^-)道中，利用Faddeev方程的Fixed Center Approximation（固定中心近似）描述πDD*三体动力学并求解全三体振幅。

Result: 三体振幅在πT_cc阈值略上方出现显著增强，峰位约M_peak=4055 MeV，表观宽度约60 MeV；振幅实部与虚部行为与共振解释相符。

Conclusion: 最轻的强子（π介子）可发挥主动动力学作用，将已观测的强子分子态提升为更重近阈值结构的构建单元。建议在T_ccπ不变质量谱中寻找该增强，特别是通过D^0D^0π^+π^-末态进行实验探测。

Abstract: Hadronic molecules are commonly viewed as products of near-threshold two-body dynamics. We investigate whether an experimentally established molecule can also serve as a building block for a more complex exotic hadron by studying pion scattering off $T^+_{cc}(3875)$. Treating $T_{cc}$ as a correlated isoscalar $D D^*$ molecular cluster, we describe the resulting $πD D^*$ dynamics in the $I(J^P)=1(1^-)$ channel within the Fixed Center Approximation to the Faddeev equations. The full three-body amplitude develops a pronounced enhancement slightly above the $πT_{cc}$ threshold, centered at $M_{\rm peak}\simeq 4055$~MeV, with an apparent width of approximately $60$~MeV. The associated behavior of the real and imaginary parts of the amplitude is compatible with a resonant-like interpretation. These results show that the lightest hadron can play an active dynamical role in promoting an observed hadronic molecule into a building block of a heavier near-threshold structure generated by the three-body dynamics. We propose searching for this enhancement in the $T_{cc}π$ invariant-mass distribution, particularly through the $D^0D^0π^+π^-$ final state.

</details>


### [127] [Kinematic Fingerprints of a Nucleon-Triggered $V_{\mathcal{B}}$ in Rare $η^{(\prime)}\toπ^0(η)γγ$ Decays on Nucleon Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13537)
*Yaroslav Balytskyi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究核子触发的矢量玻色子模型V_B，该模型源于KLOE与MAMI对η→π⁰γγ衰变率测量的显著差异。模型由外部核子激活新相互作用，只影响光产生过程而不影响轻子测量。研究发现MAMI和JEF能区存在微小但非零的残差效应，最清晰的判别特征是γ-γ和π⁰-γ谱中的运动学禁止尾部，并预测了相应的事件数及质量位移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KLOE测量的η→π⁰γγ分支比(0.98±0.11±0.14)×10⁻⁴与世界平均值(2.55±0.22)×10⁻⁴之间存在统计显著的不一致，前者由MAMI光产生数据主导。需要提出一个能够同时解释这两种测量而不与其他轻子测量冲突的新物理模型。

Method: 采用核子触发的矢量玻色子模型V_B，新相互作用仅由外部核子激活，因此不影响KLOE和BESIII等轻子测量。定量计算了残差运动学和截断依赖效应，比较了MAMI与JEF不同产生能区下的预测差异，并分析了运动学禁止尾部的事件数、反冲质子关联以及重建质量位移等可观测特征。

Result: MAMI与JEF预测彼此接近但不完全相同；在1200个样本中预测运动学禁止区域有4-23个事件；相对壳上假设的极角偏差在MAMI能量下可达约-5°或更低，在JEF下仅约-0.2°；JEF有效分支比超过MAMI预测不到1%，加宽窗口则两者增加至多约4%；两个能区均产生约2-7 MeV的平均重建质量上移；识别了V_B诱导拓扑可解释γd→π⁰ηd和γd→π⁰π⁰d角分布差异。

Conclusion: V_B模型为KLOE与MAMI之间的分支比矛盾提供了可行解释，其最清晰的实验判别是运动学禁止尾部的存在及其与反冲质子运动学的关联。模型对两个能区的预测差异很小，但对角分布异常的贡献值得未来定量研究。

Abstract: The nucleon-triggered vector-boson scenario $V_{\mathcal B}$ was motivated by a statistically significant discrepancy between the KLOE measurement, $\mathrm{BR}^{η\rightarrowπ^{0}γγ}_{\text{KLOE}} = \left(0.98\pm0.11_{\text{stat}}\pm0.14_{\text{syst}}\right)\times10^{-4}$, and the current world average, $\left(2.55\pm0.22\right)\times10^{-4}$, dominated by MAMI photoproduction data. In this model, the new interaction is activated by external nucleons, which causes deviations from the Standard Model predictions, while leaving leptonic measurements, such as KLOE and BESIII, unaffected. We quantify the residual kinematic and cut-dependent effects that make the MAMI- and JEF-like predictions close but not identical due to their different production-energy regimes. The cleanest discriminator of the model is a kinematically forbidden tail in the $γγ$ and $π^0γ$ spectra, and for $η\rightarrowπ^0γγ$, and we predict 4--23 events in a sample of 1200, depending on the selection window, in these regions. These events correlate with the kinematics of the recoil proton, and the polar angle relative to the on-shell hypothesis can reach $\approx-5^\circ$ or below at MAMI energies, but only $\approx -0.2^\circ$ at JEF. For identical selection windows, the JEF-like effective branching fraction exceeds the MAMI-like prediction by less than $1\%$, while widening the window increases both predictions by up to $\sim4\%$. Both energy regimes produce a similar upward shift of the mean reconstructed mass, $\langle M_{π^0γγ}\rangle-m_η\simeq 2$--$7~\mathrm{MeV}$. Finally, we identify representative $V_{\mathcal B}$-induced topologies that could contribute to recently reported discrepancies in the angular distributions of $γd\toπ^0ηd$ and $γd\toπ^0π^0 d$, leaving their quantitative investigation to future work.

</details>

### [128] [A Lapse in the Cosmological Constant Problem with Bulk Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12460)
*Justin Khoury,Benjamin Muntz,Antonio Padilla*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文扩展了基于各向异性缩放的宇宙学常数问题解决方案，从超局域z=0极限推广到引入z=1形变的情况。研究表明真空能量抵消在背景方程层面仍然成立，但需要Fierz-Pauli关系来消除额外标量鬼场。该框架可由五维爱因斯坦引力、拓扑形式通量和类空khoron标量场协变实现，但物质沿紧致维度传播产生的Casimir贡献不能被自动消除，需要额外的谱条件来抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学常数问题一直是理论物理的重大难题。作者此前提出了基于紧致额外维中各向异性缩放的新方法，在超局域z=0极限下能够使引力场方程对物质真空能的辐射修正不敏感。本文的动机是将这一框架推广到z=1形变，恢复紧致方向的动力学，从而构建更完整、更现实的模型。

Method: 作者在超局域极限之外引入z=1形变以恢复紧致方向的动力学。通过分析背景方程中真空能量抵消的持续性，检查外曲率耦合的二次谱，确立Fierz-Pauli关系以保证谱的健康性。在协变表述中，利用五维爱因斯坦引力、拓扑形式通量和类空khoron标量场（通过辅助einbein定义全局约束）来实现该机制。最后，考虑物质在紧致维度周围传播时的Casimir效应。

Result: 真空能量抵消在背景方程层面仍然成立；一般的曲率耦合传播额外标量鬼场，而Fierz-Pauli关系保证了健康的二次谱。协变公式中，重整化物质真空能的常量平移从内禀爱因斯坦方程中代数抵消。但物质沿紧致方向传播产生有限且拓扑敏感的Casimir贡献，不能自动消除，需额外谱条件抑制。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了从超局域极限到动力学极限的自然扩展，并保持了宇宙学常数问题的核心解决机制。然而，Casimir贡献的存在意味着要使方案完全自洽，需要对传播的体自由度施加额外的谱条件，这表明该方案虽有前景但仍有需要进一步细化的部分。

Abstract: We have previously proposed a new approach to the cosmological constant problem based on anisotropic scaling in a compact extra dimension. In the ultra-local $z=0$ limit, the interplay between a projectable lapse, foliation-preserving diffeomorphisms, and higher-form fluxes renders the gravitational field equations insensitive to radiative corrections to the matter vacuum energy. Here we extend this framework beyond the ultra-local limit by introducing $z=1$ deformations that restore dynamics along the compact direction. We show that vacuum-energy cancellation persists at the level of the background equations, while generic extrinsic-curvature couplings propagate an additional scalar ghost. A healthy quadratic spectrum selects the Fierz-Pauli relation between these couplings. This motivates a particularly simple realization in terms of five-dimensional Einstein gravity, a top-form flux, and a spacelike khoron scalar field that dynamically defines a preferred projectable foliation. In this covariant formulation, the global constraint arises from an auxiliary einbein on the space of khoron leaves, and constant shifts of the renormalized matter vacuum energy cancel algebraically from the intrinsic Einstein equations. Finally, allowing matter to propagate around the compact dimension generates finite, topology-sensitive Casimir contributions that are not automatically removed by the mechanism. Suppressing these contributions requires additional spectral conditions on the propagating bulk degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [129] [Stabilizer complexity and the Python's lunch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12472)
*Abhirup Bhattacharya,Jatin Narde,Onkar Parrikar,Suprakash Paul*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了全息CFT中部分纠缠热(PET)态约化密度矩阵的稳定子复杂度（stabilizer complexity），发现Wigner负性（一种魔性单调量）为exp[(A_out - A_min)/8G_N]，其复杂度在存在"蟒蛇午餐"（python's lunch）几何时呈指数增强，否则为O(1)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解全息纠缠楔中的量子计算复杂性，特别是从量子资源理论角度量化约化密度矩阵的"魔性"（magic），并将之与体几何结构（外极值面与内极值面的面积差）联系起来。

Method: 利用CFT谱的伪随机性假设和重算符插入假设，计算PET态相对于微正则密度矩阵的Wigner负性，并将其与体空间中的外极值面面积A_out和最小极值面面积A_min之差关联起来。

Result: Wigner负性为exp[(A_out - A_min)/8G_N]。无蟒蛇午餐时稳定子复杂度为O(1)，存在蟒蛇午餐时呈指数增强（由A_out - A_min给出指数增强）。

Conclusion: 稳定子复杂度与全息体几何中"蟒蛇午餐"结构直接相关，揭示了量子计算复杂度与时空几何（特别是纠缠楔的拓扑结构）之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: In this note, we study the stabilizer complexity of the reduced density matrix corresponding to one side of a partially entangled thermal (PET) state with fixed energy boundary conditions in a holographic CFT. In particular, we study Wigner negativity, an operationally meaningful magic monotone which can be interpreted as the complexity of classically simulating any quantum circuit preparation of the reduced state on the subregion. Using assumptions on the pseudorandomness of the CFT spectrum and the heavy operator insertion, we observe that the Wigner negativity of the PET state relative to the microcanonical density matrix at the given energy is given by $\exp\left[\frac{1}{8G_N}(A_{\text{out}} - A_{\text{min}})\right]$, where $A_{\text{out}}$ is the area of the outer extremal surface, while $A_{\text{min}}$ is the area of the minimal extremal surface. Thus, the stabilizer complexity of the reduced density matrix on the boundary subregion is $O(1)$ in the absence of a python's lunch, but gets exponentially enhanced in the presence of a python's lunch in the bulk geometry.

</details>


### [130] [Constraints on the $O(n)$ model from a negative number of flavors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12481)
*Daniele Artico,Jasper Roosmale Nepveu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过O(n)模型与Sp(-n)对偶，将算子谱的非微扰约束扩展到负偶数n值，利用不可约表示分解揭示任意n下不同算子标度维度的新简并性，从而无需额外环计算即可推导任意φ^k型算子的两环反常维度，并在特定算子基下得到新的非重整化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在O(n)模型中，将味道数n视为连续变量会导致算子谱出现非微扰约束。作者希望借助O(n)与Sp(-n)的对偶关系，将这些约束扩展到负偶数n，并通过不可约表示的分解将其显式化，从而在微扰论中揭示新的算符简并结构，简化反常维度的计算。

Method: 利用O(n)和Sp(-n)之间的对偶关系，将一般味道结构的算子分解为不可约表示；在微扰论中利用这种结构揭示不同算子标度维度的简并性；基于这些简并性从已有的结果推导任意φ^k型算子的两环反常维度；分析重整化群混合矩阵的模式以得到非重整化结果，并讨论扩展到OPE系数及时空维度延拓下的演变约束。

Result: 揭示了任意n下不同算子尺度维度的新简并性；无需额外环路计算即可从现有结果推导任意φ^k型算子的两环反常维度；在特定算子基下获得了新的非重整化结果；提出了将框架扩展到OPE系数与时空维度延续下的演变约束的途径。

Conclusion: O(n)/Sp(-n)对偶结合不可约表示分解为理解算子谱提供了强大的非微扰约束工具，能够在微扰论中简化高环计算并揭示新的对称性结构，且该框架有望推广至OPE系数与演变约束等领域。

Abstract: Treating the number of flavors $n$ as a variable in the $O(n)$ model leads to non-perturbative constraints on the spectrum of operators. Using the duality between $O(n)$ and $Sp(-n)$, we extend these relations to negative even values of $n$ and we make them explicit by decomposing operators with general flavor structure into irreducible representations. In perturbation theory, we exploit this structure to reveal novel degeneracies in the scaling dimensions of different operators, which persist for arbitrary $n$. This allows us to derive the two-loop anomalous dimension of any $φ^k$-type operator from existing results without additional loop calculations. The same mechanism dictates patterns in renormalization group mixing matrices, yielding new non-renormalization results in a specific operator basis. We comment on extending this framework to relations between operator product expansion coefficients and to analogous constraints arising from evanescence under continuation in the number of spacetime dimensions.

</details>


### [131] [Edge physics and the Casimir interaction in Maxwell--Chern--Simons theory on a strip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12622)

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Symanzik边界量子场论框架下研究条带上的Maxwell-Chern-Simons理论，推导了由局域性和变分原理确定的允许边界条件，发现条带支持两个边界U(1)流代数，其能级由Chern-Simons耦合κ和边界取向决定。文中还给出了体模反射系数和有限相互作用能的闭式行列式（散射）表示，揭示了Maxwell极限下的长程幂律Casimir力与MCS理论下拓扑质量的Yukawa抑制机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解具有边界的规范理论在条带几何中的边界条件、边界流代数结构以及边界对体物理（如Casimir效应）的影响，是拓扑物态和边界量子场论中的核心问题。作者希望通过系统性的Symanzik框架分析，厘清拓扑边界数据与动力学体效应之间的区分。

Method: 采用Symanzik的边界量子场论框架，在条带M=R^(1,1)×[0,h]上研究Maxwell-Chern-Simons理论。在二次单切向导数截断下添加最一般的局域边界泛函，通过变分原理和局域性导出允许的边界条件，利用边界Ward恒等式实现规范对称性。分析条带上的两个边界U(1)流代数及其能级与边界取向的关系，并通过行列式（散射）表示计算有限相互作用能。

Result: 条带支持两个边界U(1)流代数，能级相反并由Chern-Simons耦合κ和边界取向确定；在翻转载称对称分支上边缘速度等值反向。边界数据决定了体模反射系数，并产生闭合的行列式散射表示。Maxwell极限(κ=0)下边缘间相互作用为长程并产生幂律Casimir力；而MCS理论中拓扑质量m=κg²在mh≫1时导致Yukawa抑制。行列式形式还能区分拓扑边界数据（能级与anomaly inflow）与动力学体效应（色散、边界混合和Casimir相互作用）。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了条带上Maxwell-Chern-Simons理论中边界条件、边界流代数与体散射之间的系统联系，为边界量子场论中的拓扑与动力学效应提供了统一的解析描述，尤其在Casimir力从长程幂律到Yukawa短程行为的交叉上有精确的控制。

Abstract: We study Maxwell--Chern--Simons theories on a strip $\mathcal M=\mathbb R^{1,1}\times[0,h]$ within the Symanzik framework for quantum field theory with boundaries. We add the most general local boundary functional within the quadratic one-tangential-derivative truncation adopted throughout the paper and derive the admissible boundary conditions from locality and the variational principle, with gauge symmetry implemented through boundary Ward identities. The strip supports two boundary $U(1)$ current algebras, with opposite levels fixed by the Chern--Simons coupling $κ$ and by boundary orientation, while edge velocities depend on local boundary couplings. They are equal and opposite on the flip-symmetric branch, which is the natural choice for a spatially symmetric strip. The same boundary data determine the reflection coefficients for bulk modes and yield a closed determinant (scattering) representation of the finite interaction energy. In the Maxwell limit ($κ=0$) the inter-edge interaction is long-ranged and produces a power-law Casimir force, whereas in Maxwell--Chern--Simons theory the topological mass $m=κg^2$ generates Yukawa suppression at large separations $mh\gg1$. The determinant form also gives analytic control of the asymptotic regimes and separates topological boundary data (levels and anomaly inflow) from dynamical bulk effects (dispersion, boundary mixing and Casimir interaction).

</details>


### [132] [Type III von Neumann Algebras are Magical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12512)
*Mudassir Moosa*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文论证了Type III von Neumann代数本质上需要无穷多的magic资源（非Clifford门），这意味着在热力学极限下若态只具有有界的magic，则其局部von Neumann代数不可能是Type III的，对量子场论的量子模拟有直接影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在容错量子计算中，非Clifford门（magic）是核心资源。与此同时，描述无穷维量子系统（如量子场论）需要von Neumann代数理论，其中Type III代数不同于有限维系统中的标准框架（密度矩阵、trace等均失效）。作者希望建立magic资源与Type III代数结构之间的深层联系。

Method: 作者考虑有限维量子系统（如格点系统）的热力学极限，证明若热力学极限下的态仅具有有界的magic量，则对应的局部von Neumann代数不可能为Type III类型。通过这一约束关系，将magic资源的有限性与代数类型的拓扑分类联系起来。

Result: 证明了Type III von Neumann代数必然要求无穷大量的magic资源；有界magic的态所对应的局部von Neumann代数不能是Type III类型。该结果对量子场论的局部子区域量子模拟有直接实际意义，因为那些区域的代数已知为Type III。

Conclusion: Type III von Neumann代数在结构上内在地需要无穷多magic资源，这为理解量子场论量子模拟所需的资源开销提供了新的理论依据——模拟量子场论局部区域本质上需要消耗无穷的magic资源。

Abstract: The number of non-Clifford gates needed to perform a task, or simply \textit{magic}, is a resource for fault-tolerant quantum computation. Von Neumann algebras provide a formal mathematical structure to describe infinite-dimensional quantum systems, such as those in quantum field theory or quantum statistical mechanics. A particularly important class of von Neumann algebras is called Type III algebras, for which the standard notions of finite-dimensional systems such as density matrices and traces break down. In this work, we argue that Type III von Neumann algebras fundamentally require an infinite amount of magic. Specifically, we consider the thermodynamic limit of finite-dimensional quantum systems, such as lattice systems, and show that if the states in the thermodynamic limit possess only a bounded amount of magic, the resulting local von Neumann algebra cannot be of Type III. Our result has direct implications for the quantum simulations of quantum field theories, for which the algebra of a local subregion is known to be of Type III.

</details>


### [133] [D4-branes wrapped on topological disks from matter-coupled F(4) gauged supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12850)
*Patharadanai Nuchino,Parinya Karndumri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了物质耦合F(4)规范超引力中一类具有拓扑圆盘（半纺锤体）和U(1)全纯的AdS_4×Σ超对称解，这些解保留8个超电荷，可嵌入massive type IIA理论，在十维中对应D4-D8膜系统包裹在Σ上。部分解渐近到局域AdS_6，可视为五维N=2 SCFT中的余维2共形缺陷；存在有限与无限全息自由能两类解，扩展了已知解并提供了新解类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在系统地构造和研究AdS_4×Σ超对称解，其中Σ为具有非平凡U(1)全纯的拓扑圆盘或"半纺锤体"，以理解三维N=2 SCFT与五维N=2 SCFT之间的全息对应关系，特别是通过半纺锤体紧致化实现的维度约化机制，以及余维2共形缺陷的结构。

Method: 采用SO(3)×SO(3)规范群的物质耦合F(4)规范超引力，将其约化到U(1)×U(1)子扇区，通过研究保留8个超电荷的真空解，构造AdS_4×Σ解。将解提升到十维（嵌入massive type IIA理论），解释为D4-D8膜系统包裹在Σ上的构型，并分析解的渐近行为、全息自由能及对称性结构（SO(2)×SO(2)或SO(2)_diag）。

Result: 构造了一类新的AdS_4×Σ解，保留8个超电荷并具有SO(2)×SO(2)或SO(2)_diag对称性。部分解渐近到局域AdS_6，对应于五维N=2 SCFT中的余维2共形缺陷，其全息自由能发散；另一些解具有有限全息自由能。这些结果既推广了已有解，也提供了全新的解类。

Conclusion: 所构造的AdS_4×半纺锤体解为三维N=2 SCFT（来自五维N=2 SCFT在半纺锤体上的紧致化）提供了全息对偶。研究揭示了解的全息自由能行为与渐近几何之间的关联，并证明该框架同时涵盖广义化的已知解和全新解类，为理解高维SCFT的共形缺陷结构和维度约化提供了重要的全息工具。

Abstract: We study a number of supersymmetric $AdS_4\times Σ$ solutions with $Σ$ being a topological disk with non-trivial $U(1)$ holonomy on the boundary or a ``half-spindle'' from matter-coupled $F(4)$ gauged supergravity. The gauged supergravity is coupled to three vector multiplets with $SO(3)\times SO(3)$ gauge group. The resulting solutions preserve eight supercharges and $SO(2)\times SO(2)$ or $SO(2)_{\text{diag}}$ symmetries and are expected to be dual to $N=2$ SCFTs in three dimensions arising from compactifications of five-dimensional $N=2$ SCFT on a half-spindle. All of the solutions lie within the $U(1)\times U(1)$ subsector of the matter-coupled $F(4)$ gauged supergravity that can be embedded in massive type IIA theory. After uplifted to ten dimensions, the solutions can be interpreted as a system of D4-D8-branes wrapped on $Σ$. Some of the solutions are asymptotic to a locally $AdS_6$ geometry and can be identified as codimension-2 conformal defects within the $N=2$ SCFT in five dimensions. For these solutions, the circle inside $Σ$ decompactifies in the $AdS_6$ limit rendering the holographic free energy infinite. There also exist solutions with finite free energy in the dual three-dimensional SCFTs. In addition, we show that the results both extend the previously known solutions and provide a novel class of solutions.

</details>


### [134] [Limits of the inverse scattering problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13105)
*Matvei Fedin,Kirill Gubarev,Andrey Morozov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究粒子层析成像问题，探讨在粒子速度受限条件下，如何确定速度下限以从线积分测量中重建势函数，使用理论示例和机器学习方法验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统层析成像通过线积分重建密度函数（即Radon逆变换），但将其推广到粒子情形时需要限制粒子速度，否则问题退化为经典情况。因此需要探究粒子速度可低到什么程度，取决于势函数的参数。

Method: 使用简单理论示例分析粒子速度下限与势函数参数的关系，并构建机器学习流水线来恢复势函数以验证理论分析。

Result: 确定了粒子速度下限与所研究势函数参数之间的依赖关系，并通过机器学习方法成功实现了势函数的恢复。

Conclusion: 粒子层析成像是可行的，其速度下限取决于势函数的具体参数；机器学习方法可作为恢复势函数的有效工具。

Abstract: The main goal of tomography is the reconstruction of density function out of its line integrals (integral measurements of this density along x-ray lines). Such construction is possible and known as inverse x-ray/Radon transformations. We are interested in generalization of this problem to the case of particles. For this we need to limit the speed of these particles, otherwise the problem is reduced to the previous one. The question we discuss in this paper is how low can this speed be depending on the parameters of the studied potential. We study this problem using simple theoretical examples and Machine Learning pipeline to restore the potential.

</details>


### [135] [Exact Evaluation of Lattice-Regularized Scalar Field Vacuum Amplitude via Site Permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13117)
*Vadim Asnin*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法计算任意维度、任意势的标量场真空振幅在晶格上存在困难，需要一种新的计算途径来简化或解决这一问题。

Method: 路径积分中动能指数因子首先在所有晶格位点排列下进行对称化处理，然后对对称化后的表达式应用Waring分解（即将对称多项式分解为线性形式的幂次之和）来完成真空振幅的计算。

Result: 该方法适用于任意维度、任意势能的单个标量场，在晶格上能够系统地计算真空振幅。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种通用的、可适用于任意维度和任意势的晶格标量场真空振幅计算方法，为相关量子场论问题提供了新的求解工具。

Abstract: A novel approach to a computation of vacuum amplitude for a single scalar field in any number of dimensions with arbitrary potential on a lattice is proposed. The computation involves symmetrization of kinetic exponential factor in the path integral over all permutations of lattice sites and Waring decomposition of the symmetrized expression.

</details>
