*W. A. Sabra,R. Slim*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文为一大类耦合标量场和阿贝尔规范场的D维引力理论构建了四族依赖单一变量的时空度量族，并应用于无规范N=2, D=4超引力、N=8超引力的一致截断以及标量场参数化对称陪集流形SL(N,R)/SO(N,R)的理论，得到显式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为广泛的引力理论（含标量和规范场）提供统一的单变量时空度量的构造框架，并通过超引力理论的应用验证该形式体系的普适性，揭示标量流形几何在解结构中的核心作用。

Method: 构造四族依赖单一变量的时空度量族，推广到D维引力理论，系统中包含标量场和阿贝尔规范场。利用标量流形的几何结构（如对称陪集流形）来确定解的形式，并应用于不同超引力理论得到显式解。

Result: 得到了包含非平凡标量和规范场位形的显式解，覆盖了无规范N=2,D=4超引力与矢量多重态的耦合、N=8超引力的一致截断，以及SL(N,R)/SO(N,R)陪集流形理论等多种情形。

Conclusion: 标量流形的几何结构对决定解的拓扑和结构起核心作用，所建立的统一形式体系可广泛应用于D维引力理论中单变量解的构造。

Abstract: We construct four families of spacetime metrics depending on a single variable for a broad class of $D$-dimensional gravitational theories coupled to scalar and Abelian gauge fields. As applications of the general formalism, we derive one-variable solutions of ungauged $\mathcal{N}=2$, $D=4$ supergravity coupled to vector multiplets. We also obtain explicit solutions for a consistent truncation of $\mathcal{N}=8$, $D=4$ supergravity, as well as for theories whose scalar fields parametrize the symmetric coset manifolds $SL(N,\mathbb{R})/SO(N,\mathbb{R})$. In all cases, the geometry of the scalar manifold plays a central role in determining the structure of the resulting solutions with nontrivial scalar and gauge field configurations.

</details>


### [137] [Lee-Yang paradigm of phase transition in eigenstate thermalized systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13174)
*Yongjiang Xu,Weixin Sun,Chushun Tian,Huajia Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文展示了在全息共形场论中，即使系统呈现本征态热化，也能发生属于Lee-Yang范式的热力学类相变。通过将本征态自关联函数的期望映射到虚拟相互作用瞬子气体的配分函数，作者发现当中心荷c→∞时，一对复共轭零点移动到实轴上的著名禁戒奇点处，系统穿过该奇点从低逸度相跃迁到高逸度相，伴随自由能标度行为和微观构型的戏剧性变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孤立量子系统中的相变一直难以捉摸，作者旨在探索在展示本征态热化的系统中是否以及如何出现热力学类相变，以揭示本征态热化与相变物理之间更深层的联系。

Method: 作者将全息共形场论中本征态的自关联函数期望映射到虚拟相互作用瞬子气体的配分函数Z_gauge(z)，其中虚时进行共形映射z=1-e^{-τ}，中心荷c扮演瞬子逸度的角色。通过分析配分函数零点的运动轨迹，参照Lee-Yang相变范式来研究相变行为。

Result: 发现当c→∞时，配分函数的一对复共轭零点移动到实轴上的著名禁戒奇点处。系统穿过该奇点时从低逸度相跃迁至高逸度相，同时自由能的标度行为和主导微观构型均发生显著变化。

Conclusion: 本征态热化系统中的相变物理非常丰富，热力学类相变可以以Lee-Yang范式出现在展示本征态热化的全息共形场论中。

Abstract: As phase transitions in isolated quantum systems remain elusive, here we show how a thermodynamic-like phase transition, falling into the Lee-Yang paradigm, can arise in systems displaying eigenstate thermalization. Specifically, we show that in holographic conformal field theories, the eigenstate expectation of the auto-correlation function can be mapped to the partition function ${\cal Z}_{gauge}(z)$ of a virtual interacting instanton gas, with the conformal mapping of the imaginary time: $z=1-e^{-τ}$ and the central charge $c$ mimicking the instanton fugacity and volume, respectively. We find that akin to the Lee-Yang paradigm, for $c\to\infty$ a pair of complex conjugate zeros of ${\cal Z}_{gauge}(z)$ move to the real axis located at the famous forbidden singularity. Passing through the singularity the system transits from the low- to high-fugacity phase, accompanied by dramatic changes in scaling behaviors of the free energy and dominant microscopic configurations. Our findings indicate that physics of phase transitions from eigenstate thermalization is very rich.

</details>


### [138] [Phase transition from eigenstate thermalization: forbidden singularity and instanton proliferation via AGT correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13246)
*Yongjiang Xu,Weixin Sun,Chushun Tian,Huajia Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用AGT对偶，将2D CFT中自相关函数的禁止奇点（本征态热化的关键特征）与N=2超对称规范理论中瞬子气体的相变联系起来。在重轻极限的对偶对应下，瞬子气体出现两个相，分别由不同结构的弦状Young图主导，其性质类似禁闭与退禁闭相；相变遵循Lee-Yang理论，临界逸度恰好对应禁止奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在理论物理中，发现不同背景下问题之间的联系是推动进展的重要方式。本文旨在将2D CFT中本征态热化的关键信号——禁止奇点——与相变现象联系起来，通过AGT对应这一桥梁揭示二者深层的数学物理联系。

Method: 利用AGT对应，将本征态自关联函数映射为N=2 SUSY规范理论中描述瞬子气体的Nekrasov配分函数。通过取与重轻极限对应的对偶极限，分析瞬子气体的两个相，识别出由弦状Young图主导的不同构型及其热力学性质，并借助Lee-Yang理论分析相变及临界逸度。

Result: 瞬子气体在对应重轻极限的对偶设定下涌现出两个相，分别由结构不同的弦状Young图主导，具有截然不同的热力学性质，与禁闭相和退禁闭相相似。相变随瞬子增殖发生，机制类似Lee-Yang理论；计算得到的临界逸度与CFT中禁止奇点的位置精确对应。

Conclusion: 本文建立了禁止奇点与瞬子气体相变之间的精确对应关系，揭示了本征态热化现象与统计力学相变理论之间的深层联系，为理解CFT中禁止奇点的物理本质提供了全新的视角。

Abstract: In theoretical physics, finding connections between problems that appear in distinct contexts is an important way to leapfrog progresses, often by illuminating deep aspects that may otherwise seem obscure. In this paper, we consider in 2d CFTs the phenomenon of forbidden singularities in auto-correlation functions -- a key signature of eigenstate thermalization. We show that they correspond to phase transitions in the context of eigenstates. The connection is made explicit by utilizing the AGT correspondence, which relates eigenstate auto-correlations to the Nekrasov partition functions describing an instanton gas of the $\mathcal{N}=2$ SUSY gauge theories. We show that by taking the counter-part of the heavy-light limit, two phases emerge for the instanton gas. They are dominated by configurations represented by string-like Young tableaux with distinct structures and thermodynamic properties, which bare resemblance to the confined and the deconfined phases. A phase transition occurs as instantons proliferate from one side, in a manner that mimics the Lee-Yang theory. We work out the critical fugacity and find it corresponding exactly to the forbidden singularity.

</details>


### [139] [3d $\mathcal{N}=$ 4 rank-0 SCFT from punctured lens space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13300)
*Sungjoon Kim*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从两个平行M5膜在带单根去除顶点的透镜空间L(2n+3,2)上的紧致化出发，推导出Gang-Kim-Stubbs理论T_n（即M(2n+3,2) Virasoro最小模型的3d N=4秩零超共形场论的拓扑A扭结描述）。在此基础上，提出一族来自带穿刺的L(2n+3,1)透镜空间的3d N=2阿贝尔规范理论，其红外相位实现M(2n+3,2)模张量范畴的Galois轨道中的幺正成员，并猜想透镜空间的双手征性条件给出自镜像秩零不动点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从几何工程化的角度建立Gang-Kim-Stubbs理论（3d N=4秩零SCFT）的统一起源，并将其推广到更一般的透镜空间族，以探索Virasoro最小模型模张量范畴的Galois轨道结构以及自镜像秩零不动点的存在性。

Method: 利用M5膜在透镜空间上的紧致化作为几何工程化框架：先从L(2n+3,2)（去掉一个顶点）推导出已知的GKS理论T_n，再推广到带穿刺的L(2n+3,1)透镜空间构造3d N=2阿贝尔规范理论，并通过透镜空间的双手征性（amphichirality）条件提出自镜像秩零不动点的猜想。

Result: 提出了一族新的3d N=2阿贝尔规范理论，其红外不动点实现M(2n+3,2)模张量范畴Galois轨道中幺正成员的物理实现；同时提出自镜像秩零不动点的存在性猜想。

Conclusion: 该工作将Gang-Kim-Stubbs理论置于统一的透镜空间M5膜紧致化框架中，并开辟了通往2d最小模型Galois轨道新成员的3d场论实现路径，同时通过双手征性提出自镜像秩零理论的新猜想。

Abstract: {\it Gang-Kim-Stubbs} theory $\mathcal{T}_n$ --- a pioneering 3d bulk description of $M(2n+3,2)$ Virasoro minimal model as $\mathcal{N}=4$ rank-0 superconformal field theory upon topological A-twist --- is derived from the compactification of a pair of parallel M5-branes on {\it lens space} $L(2n+3,2)$ with a single vertex removed. From this perspective, we propose a family of 3d $\mathcal{N}=2$ abelian gauge theories arising from the punctured $L(2n+3,1)$ lens space whose infrared phases realize the unitary member in the Galois orbit of $M(2n+3,2)$ modular tensor category. We also conjecture self-mirror rank-0 fixed points from amphichirality condition of the lens space.

</details>


### [140] [Frame-dependency of the confinement temperature in a strongly-coupled plasma under rotation: a holographic description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13301)
*Nelson R. F. Braga,Alexsandre L. Ferreira*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过最一般的Myers-Perry黑洞解来推广先前关于旋转夸克-胶子等离子体（QGP）临界温度的框架依赖性的全息研究。研究发现，打破等角动量情况下的球对称性后，共转观测者测得的局域温度具有非平凡的角依赖关系：随着旋转速度增加，温度可以降低、升高甚至呈现非单调行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究发现，格点计算与全息模型关于QGP旋转效应的分歧源于参考系选择不同：静态观测者测得禁闭温度随旋转降低，而共转系中则相反。理解临界温度对参考系的依赖性是描述旋转QGP的重要步骤，但此前研究局限于等角动量（球对称）情形，需推广到更具一般性的情况。

Method: 将QGP的描述推广到最一般的Myers-Perry黑洞解，该解打破了先前等角动量情况下的球对称性，从而引入非平凡的角依赖结构。通过在该背景下计算局域温度的角分布行为，来分析不同旋转速度下的临界温度变化规律。

Result: 与非球对称情形不同，共转观测者测得的局域温度呈现出丰富的角依赖行为：随着旋转速度增加，局域温度可能在角度方向的不同位置上分别表现为下降、上升或非单调变化。

Conclusion: 将全息模型推广到最一般的Myers-Perry黑洞解后，旋转QGP的临界温度不仅依赖于参考系，还依赖于观测位置的角度方向。这意味着旋转QGP的禁闭/退禁闭相变特性比此前认识到的更加复杂，需要更细致的描述框架。

Abstract: Recently, it was demonstrated that the disagreement between lattice calculations and holographic models, regarding rotational effects in the quark-gluon plasma (QGP), occurs due to different choices of reference frames. While a static observer measures a confinement temperature that decreases with rotation, one in a co-rotating frame finds the opposite behavior. Both results are correct, and a comprehension of the frame-dependency of the critical temperature is a significant step in the description of the QGP under rotation. In this article, we generalize this previous holographic result by describing the plasma through the most general Myers-Perry black hole solution. This breaks the spherical symmetry present in the equal angular momentum case, considered before. As a consequence, the local temperature measured by a co-rotating observer has a non-trivial angular dependence: it can decrease, increase or even behave non-monotonically when rotational velocity increases.

</details>


### [141] [False-vacuum bubbles in sphaleron scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11370)
*Manuel A. Martínez Sánchez,Christoph Adam,Danial Saadatmand*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了(1+1)维变形φ^6标量场理论中两个亮sphaleron的碰撞动力学，发现丰富多样的最终态（如kink-反kink对、真/假真空中的振荡子、假真空中传播的多振荡子、辐射衰变等），特别值得注意的是出现了被kink-反kink对包围的长时间存活假真空气泡，反复塌缩和膨胀后才最终衰变为振荡子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究具有假真空的变形φ^6场论中不稳定局域构型（sphaleron）的碰撞动力学，揭示其比先前认知更丰富的非线性行为，深化对不稳定局部构型在非线性场论中角色的理解。

Method: 采用两种单参数实现模型（势垒模型和井模型），首先回顾其静态性质和线性不稳定性，然后对加速的sphaleron在大范围初始速度和形变参数下进行对头碰撞的数值模拟研究。

Result: 碰撞散射动力学展现出丰富的最终态：产生kink-反kink对、真假真空中的长寿振荡子、假真空中传播的多个振荡子，以及辐射衰变。最突出的结果是出现了由kink-反kink对束缚的长寿命假真空气泡，经过反复塌缩和再膨胀后最终衰变为振荡子。

Conclusion: 具有假真空的变形φ^6理论中的sphaleron动力学远比先前已知的更为丰富，为不稳定局域构型在非线性场论中的作用提供了新见解。

Abstract: We investigate the collision dynamics of two bright sphalerons in a (1+1)-dimensional deformed $φ^6$ scalar field theory with a symmetric potential possessing false vacua. Two one-parameter realizations of the model, referred to as the barrier and well models, are considered and their static and linear instability properties are first reviewed. We then study head-on collisions of boosted sphalerons over a broad range of initial velocities and deformation parameters. The scattering dynamics exhibit a rich variety of final states, including the production of kink-antikink pairs, long-lived oscillons in true and false vacuum, multiple oscillons propagating in false-vacuum regions, and radiative decay. A particularly remarkable outcome is the emergence of a long-lived bubble of the false broken vacuum bounded by a kink-antikink pair, which repeatedly collapses and re-expands before eventually decaying into an oscillon. These results demonstrate that deformed $φ^6$ theories with false vacua exhibit considerably richer sphaleron dynamics than previously known and provide new insight into the role of unstable localized configurations in nonlinear field theories.

</details>


### [142] [Holographic Renormalization for String-Derived Lovelock--Horndeski Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13319)
*Tianhao Wu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究五维弦导出Lovelock–Horndeski（SDLH）理论在精确线性-膨胀渐近局部AdS分支上的全息重建问题，构造了任意边界度规和时空相关标量源的重整化生成泛函，证明全息观测量可以过定重建弦紧化参数（包括无符号歧义地重建高维Gauss–Bonnet系数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦导出高曲率标量-张量引力将微观耦合数据编码在边界响应中，核心问题是：全息观测量能否反过来重建底层高维参数？作者希望回答这一可重构性问题，为弦约化提供显式的边界"指纹"。

Method: 构建边界协变的径向分层，统一处理变分问题、局域反作用、对数阻塞、有限一点函数和Ward恒等式；通过两个响应行列式组织递归、共振阻塞以及度规-标量混合；把Weyl反常压缩为Euler密度、Weyl平方密度和单个曲率-标量平方，其配对变分生成度规与标量的阻塞项。

Result: 在正则分支上，四个标量-归一化不变的全息组合对连续约化耦合存在全局有理逆映射；在固定紧化维数下该映射具有最大秩，且曲率-反常和可以无符号歧义地重建高维Gauss–Bonnet系数。

Conclusion: 全息响应提供了底层弦约化的一个显式、过定的边界指纹，证明边界几何、算子响应与反常系数中携带的弦选择耦合数据足以重建高维理论参数。

Abstract: String-derived higher-curvature scalar--tensor gravities encode microscopic coupling data in boundary response, raising the question of whether holographic observables can reconstruct the underlying higher-dimensional parameters. We answer this question for the five-dimensional string-derived Lovelock--Horndeski (SDLH) theory on its exact linear-dilaton asymptotically locally AdS branch, constructing the renormalized generating functional for an arbitrary boundary metric and spacetime-dependent scalar source. A boundary-covariant radial hierarchy unifies the variational problem, local backreaction, logarithmic obstruction, finite one-point functions, and Ward identities. Two response determinants organize the recursion, resonant obstructions, and metric--scalar mixing. The Weyl anomaly condenses into an Euler density, a Weyl-squared density, and a single curvature--scalar square whose paired variations generate the metric and scalar obstructions. The resulting renormalized functional carries string-selected coupling data into boundary geometry, operator response, anomaly coefficients, and a calculable interface with gravitational observables. On the regular branch, four scalar-normalization-invariant holographic combinations admit a global rational inverse to the continuous reduced couplings. At fixed compactification dimension the map has maximal rank, while the curvature-anomaly sum reconstructs the higher-dimensional Gauss--Bonnet coefficient without sign ambiguity. Holographic response thus provides an explicit, overdetermined boundary fingerprint of the underlying string reduction.

</details>


### [143] [Localised Horizons and Holographic Thermodynamics: Supercooling in the 1/D Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13557)
*Prateek Agrawal,Gaurang Ramakant Kane,Vazha Loladze*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了全息对偶中四维禁闭规范理论的热相变，通过将引力对偶推广到D+1维并使用1/D展开，发现了一种与标量势细节无关的普适预测结构。最大过冷度在领头阶由退禁闭相声速决定，且与多种具体模型的计算结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在全息框架中，四维禁闭规范理论通常通过五维爱因斯坦-标量引力建模，但热禁闭相变的预测依赖于标量势的具体形式。作者希望找到一种不依赖势能细节的普适结构，从而揭示相变的普适特征。

Method: 将引力对偶推广到D+1维，利用1/D展开进行解析分析。在领头阶，退禁闭相的黑膜几何效应局限于视界附近，因此可以解析构造。通过分析解的存在性确定最小温度T_min，并推导最大过冷度ε_sc的表达式。

Result: 发现解在低于最小温度T_min时不存在，最大过冷度ε_sc=1-T_min/T_c在领头阶被1/D^2因子抑制。惊人的是，领头阶最大过冷度由规范理论退禁闭相在临界温度处的声速决定，即ε_sc=c_s^2(T_c)/2。这一预测与指数超势、改进全息以及球面上N=4超Yang-Mills热相变的具体计算结果一致。

Conclusion: 这些结果与标量势细节无关，暗示热禁闭相变存在普适性。最大过冷度与临界温度处声速的简单关系为全息模型中的相变预测提供了强有力的普适约束，并得到多种具体模型的验证。

Abstract: In holography, four-dimensional confining gauge theories are often modelled by five-dimensional Einstein--scalar gravity by choosing a specific form of the scalar potential. In a large class of non-conformal theories, we show that a predictive structure emerges for the thermal confinement transition by generalising the gravitational dual to $D+1$ dimensions and using a $1/D$ expansion. These results are independent of the details of the scalar potential, hinting towards universality. The black brane geometry dual to the deconfined phase can be analytically constructed due to its effects being localised near the horizon at leading order. The solution does not exist below a minimal temperature $T_{\rm min}$ and the maximum possible supercooling in the transition $ε_{\rm sc} = 1-T_{\rm min}/T_{\rm c}$ is generically suppressed by a factor of $1/D^2$. Remarkably, the maximum supercooling at the leading order is set by the speed of sound in the deconfined phase of the gauge theory at the critical temperature, $ε_{\rm sc}=c_s^2(T_{\rm c})/2$. These predictions agree with explicit calculations in an exponential superpotential, improved holography, and the thermal transition in $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang--Mills on a sphere.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Dynamical uncertainty geometry for nonlinear transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12521)
*Giacomo Tommei*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出动态不确定性几何（DUG）框架，将后验不确定性、未来观测和模型诱导的传输分离，通过质量排序的置信过滤和传输骨架来索引后验置信集，并建立质量索引持续模块的稳定性结果，在两个基准问题中验证了该框架能识别连通不确定性区域内动态上不同的结果类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性动力系统中的不确定性往往由传输结构组织，而这些结构仅从后验几何中无法直接显现。现有方法常将后验不确定性、未来观测和模型诱导传输三个要素混为一谈，且仅在单一阈值下检查物理上有意义的类别，缺乏跨置信水平追踪的能力。

Method: DUG框架包含三个组成部分：(1) 质量排序的置信过滤（mass-ranked credible filtration）；(2) 通过诱导概率律和Fisher几何描述未来实验；(3) 表示动态不同结果的有标签传输骨架。三者通过共同的包围概率坐标统一索引。同时建立质量索引持续模块在同时扰动后验密度和概率测度下的稳定性结果，涵盖连续、加细和有限原子化公式。

Result: 在短弧轨道确定问题中，DUG识别出连通不确定区域内动态上不同的返回类别，并揭示了基于信息与基于局部Fisher观测设计标准之间的差异。在地球-月球平面圆限制三体问题中，DUG揭示了连通置信区域内多个首次命中传输结果的共存，并提供评估其数值分辨率的诊断工具。

Conclusion: 将后验几何的拓扑摘要与传输标签和未来实验耦合，能提供比单独的后验概率或动态分类更丰富的不确定性描述。稳定性结果保证了对数值近似和加权网格上持续特征的计算具有定量控制。

Abstract: Uncertainty in nonlinear dynamical systems is often organized by transport structures that are not apparent from posterior geometry alone. We introduce Dynamical Uncertainty Geometry (DUG), a framework that separates three ingredients that are frequently conflated: posterior uncertainty, future observations, and model-induced transport. DUG indexes posterior credible sets and transport-conditioned subsets by a common enclosed-probability coordinate, allowing physically meaningful classes to be tracked across credibility levels rather than examined at a single threshold. The framework combines a mass-ranked credible filtration, a description of future experiments through their induced probability laws and Fisher geometry, and a labelled transport skeleton representing dynamically distinct outcomes. We establish stability results for the resulting mass-indexed persistence modules under simultaneous perturbations of posterior density and probability measure, including continuum, refinement, and finite atomic formulations. These results provide quantitative control of persistence computed from numerical approximations and weighted grids. Two benchmark problems illustrate the framework. In a short-arc orbit-determination setting, DUG identifies dynamically distinct return classes within a connected uncertainty region and highlights a difference between information-based and local Fisher-based observation-design criteria. In the Earth-Moon planar circular restricted three-body problem, DUG reveals multiple first-hit transport outcomes coexisting within a connected credible region and provides diagnostics for assessing their numerical resolution. Together, these examples show how topological summaries of posterior geometry, when coupled to transport labels and future experiments, yield a richer description of uncertainty than either posterior probabilities or dynamical classifications alone.

</details>


### [145] [Neveu--Schwarz Irregular Vertex Operators, Decomposition Theorems, and Bilinear Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12727)
*Hajime Nagoya*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了Neveu-Schwarz代数的秩零不规则顶点算子，将其定义为同一秩的不规则Verma模之间的线性映射，满足超共形对易关系和不规则渐近条件。在非退化假设Λ₂ₚ≠0下证明其存在唯一性，并将分解定理推广到不规则情形：自由费米Fock模与秩p的NS不规则Verma模的张量积可分解为两个Virasoro不规则Verma模张量积的无限直和。同时证明不规则顶点算子与Virasoro不规则顶点算子张量积的相容分解，并推导出加权和的双线性微分方程，与量子Painlevé V和IV tau函数方程中的算子一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Virasoro代数框架下的不规则共形块和量子Painlevé方程研究推广到具有超对称结构的Neveu-Schwarz代数，建立秩零不规则顶点算子的严格数学框架，并连接不可积系统（Painlevé方程）与超共形场论。

Method: (1) 构造NS代数的秩零不规则顶点算子并证明其存在唯一性；(2) 利用分解定理将自由费米Fock模与NS不规则Verma模的张量积分解为Virasoro不规则Verma模的张量积直和；(3) 证明顶点算子在该分解下的相容分解；(4) 通过恰当的配对和模插入，推导加权和的双线性微分方程；(5) 在Painlevé V情形下进行参数识别和规范变换以匹配量子Painlevé方程。

Result: 在非退化条件下证明了秩零不规则顶点算子的存在唯一性；建立了NS不规则Verma模与Virasoro不规则Verma模之间的分解定理；导出的双线性微分算子经参数识别和规范变换后与量子Painlevé V和IV tau函数方程中的算子完全一致。

Conclusion: 该工作将不规则顶点算子的构造从Virasoro代数推广到超对称的Neveu-Schwarz代数，建立了超共形场论与量子Painlevé方程之间的桥梁，为研究NS不规则共形块及其在可积系统中的应用提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We construct rank-zero irregular vertex operators for the Neveu--Schwarz algebra as linear maps between irregular Verma modules of the same rank satisfying the usual superconformal commutation relations and an irregular asymptotic condition. Under the nondegeneracy assumption $Λ_{2p}\neq0$, we prove their existence and uniqueness. We then extend the decomposition theorem for the Neveu--Schwarz algebra to the irregular setting: the tensor product of the free-fermion Fock module with a rank-$p$ Neveu--Schwarz irregular Verma module decomposes into an infinite direct sum of tensor products of two Virasoro irregular Verma modules. We also prove a compatible decomposition of the irregular vertex operators into tensor products of Virasoro irregular vertex operators. Using suitable pairings and mode insertions, we derive bilinear differential equations for weighted sums of products of Virasoro irregular conformal blocks of types $(0,0,1)$ and $(0,2)$. After explicit parameter identifications and a gauge transformation in the Painlevé V case, the resulting bilinear differential operators agree with those appearing in the quantum Painlevé V and IV tau-function equations.

</details>


### [146] [From Lie--Rinehart Algebras to $F$-Manifold Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12802)
*Yufeng Pei,Yunhe Sheng*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为每个Lie–Rinehart代数在其基代数与模的直和上构造了一个F-流形代数，反驳了Loday–Vallette书中关于该结构为Poisson的断言，并研究了Leibnizator与Lie–Rinehart微分之间的关系以及正次数理想的Poisson理想性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lie–Rinehart代数将李代数与交换代数统一起来，是微分几何中向量场与函数环相互作用的代数抽象。作者发现此前文献（Loday–Vallette）中关于其诱导结构为Poisson代数的断言存在错误，并希望厘清其真实代数结构（即F-流形代数）及其与Leibnizator的关系。

Method: 作者在基代数A与Lie–Rinehart模L的直和A⊕L上构造F-流形代数结构，通过研究其对称Poisson代数中正次数理想幂次是否为Poisson理想来刻画该结构的性质，并将Leibnizator与Lie–Rinehart微分建立联系。对于常秩有限投射模，利用Leibnizator的迹恢复anchor映射，进而得到内射anchor的刚性结果。

Result: （1）证明A⊕L上诱导的结构一般是F-流形代数而非Poisson代数，修正了Loday–Vallette中的错误断言；（2）刻画了正次数理想幂次成为Poisson理想的条件；（3）建立了Leibnizator与Lie–Rinehart微分之间的关联；（4）对常秩有限投射模，Leibnizator的迹可恢复anchor，并得到内射anchor的刚性结论；（5）给出代数和几何方面的具体实例。

Conclusion: 文章澄清了Lie–Rinehart代数诱导结构的真实代数性质（F-流形而非Poisson），并通过Leibnizator的迹给出了anchor的代数恢复方法及刚性结果，为Lie–Rinehart代数的结构理论提供了新的工具和视角，对F-流形理论有重要推动作用。

Abstract: For every Lie--Rinehart algebra, we construct an $F$-manifold algebra on the
  direct sum of its base algebra and module. Contrary to the assertion in
  \cite[Proposition 13.3.26]{LodayVallette}, the resulting structure is generally
  not Poisson. We determine when powers of the positive-degree ideal in the
  associated symmetric Poisson algebra are Poisson ideals, and relate the
  Leibnizator to the Lie--Rinehart differential. For a finite projective module
  of constant rank, the trace of the Leibnizator recovers the anchor and yields a
  rigidity result for injective anchors. We conclude with algebraic and
  geometric examples.

</details>


### [147] [A discrete Smorodinsky--Winternitz I superintegrable system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12899)
*Vutha Vichhea Chea,Luc Vinet*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在二维方形格子的三角形区域上构造了Smorodinsky–Winternitz I超可积系统的有限离散实现，基于一对具有有限谱的交换数算子和伴随的阶梯算子结构，证明了该模型是最大超可积的，其对称代数具有Hahn代数表示，谱问题可通过Tratnik型二元对偶Hahn多项式精确求解，且连续极限能恢复原始连续模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将连续超可积系统实现为有限离散模型，是量子可积系统与离散数学物理中的重要问题，有助于理解超可积性在离散框架下的表现形式，并为数值模拟和量子计算等应用提供基础。

Method: 该构造基于一对具有有限谱的交换数算子以及与之关联的阶梯算子结构；通过分析对称代数的Hahn代数表示来证明最大超可积性；利用Tratnik型二元对偶Hahn多项式精确求解谱问题；最后通过连续极限恢复连续Smorodinsky–Winternitz I系统的各要素。

Result: 成功构造了有限离散Smorodinsky–Winternitz I系统，证明其最大超可积性，给出了Hahn代数对称结构，精确求解了谱问题，并验证了连续极限正确恢复连续模型的所有核心结构。

Conclusion: 本文建立的构造是连续Smorodinsky–Winternitz I系统的真正有限离散实现，连接了离散与连续超可积系统，为相关领域提供了一类新的精确可解模型的构建范例。

Abstract: We construct a finite discrete realization of the Smorodinsky--Winternitz I superintegrable system on a triangular region of the two-dimensional square lattice. The construction is based on a pair of commuting number operators with finite spectrum together with an associated ladder-operator structure. We show that the resulting model is maximally superintegrable and that its symmetry algebra admits a Hahn-algebra presentation. Its spectral problem is solved exactly in terms of the bivariate dual Hahn polynomials of Tratnik type. Finally, we show that the continuum limit recovers the continuous Smorodinsky--Winternitz I system together with its ladder operators, eigenfunctions and symmetry algebra, thereby establishing the present construction as a genuine finite discrete realization of the continuous model.

</details>


### [148] [Notes on Electrostatics in $\mathbb R^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12900)
*Vladimir Gol'dshtein,Wolfgang H. Müller,Reuven Segev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种紧凑的静电学表述，不依赖本构关系（包括以太关系），通过电荷势场定义电荷分布，并推导出力学力泛函，其中包含非对称主动应力场D_i E_j，其反对称部分为机械力偶密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的静电学表述依赖于本构关系（如以太关系），这可能限制了理论的一般性和物理洞察。作者希望提出一种不依赖这些假设的紧凑表述，以获得更一般的力学力表达式。

Method: 提出电荷势场来指定电荷分布，并给出带电区域在势函数下的势能表达式（不引入本构关系）。假设电荷虚运动时，场所做的虚功等于势能对时间的负导数，由此推导出机械力泛函表达式。该力泛函包含非对称主动应力场D_i E_j的作用，其反对称部分对应机械力偶密度。

Result: 推导出了机械力泛函的表达式，揭示了力泛函中包含非对称主动应力场D_i E_j，并且该应力场的反对称部分即为机械力偶密度。

Conclusion: 通过不依赖本构关系的紧凑表述，证明了静电学中力学力可由非对称应力场D_i E_j描述，其中反对称部分自然地给出机械力偶密度，为静电学提供了更一般性的理论框架。

Abstract: A compact formulation of electrostatics is presented. Without using constitutive relations, including the aether relations, an expression for the potential energy of a charged region under a potential function is proposed, where the charge distribution is specified by the charge potential field. Assuming that during a virtual motion of the charge, the virtual work expended by the field is equal to minus the time derivative of the potential energy, an expression for the mechanical force functional is derived. The force functional contains the action of an asymmetric active stress field D_{i}E_{j}, the skew-symmetric part of which is the mechanical couple density.

</details>


### [149] [A discrete Smorodinsky--Winternitz II superintegrable system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12909)
*Pierre-Antoine Bernard,Vutha Vichhea Chea,Luc Vinet*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在二维方格三角形的区域上构建了一个离散的Smorodinsky–Winternitz II超可积系统，通过一对具有有限谱的交换有限差分计数算子和阶梯算子结构实现，证明其最大超可积性，并用Krawtchouk与dual Hahn型的双变量正交多项式精确求解谱问题，同时展示了其连续极限恢复经典系统及Hahn代数在极限中的奇异化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将连续超可积系统离散化是数学物理中的重要问题。连续Smorodinsky–Winternitz II系统是经典的可分离超可积系统，但以往缺乏系统的离散版本构造。本文旨在构建这一系统的离散对应物，并在离散框架下保留其超可积性质、对称代数结构和精确可解性。

Method: 作者利用一对具有有限谱的交换有限差分计数算子构建系统的哈密顿量，其核心思想是构造伴随的阶梯算子结构，从而在二维方格上的三角区域中实现离散的SW II系统。通过引入因子化的A2-Leonard对，将谱问题化归为混合Krawtchouk型和dual Hahn型双变量正交多项式问题；对称代数的描述通过Hahn代数实现。在连续极限中，分析Hahn代数呈现的奇异化行为，并转向Laguerre–Heun代数描述。

Result: 成功构造了离散SW II超可积系统，证明其最大超可积性，其对称代数为Hahn代数。谱问题借助混合Krawtchouk/dual Hahn双变量正交多项式精确求解。连续极限恢复经典SW II系统及Hermite–Laguerre本征函数，且离散对称代数的Hahn呈现在此极限下变为奇异，极限代数结构由与Cartesian及抛物分离变量相关的Laguerre–Heun代数自然描述。

Conclusion: 本文为离散超可积系统与经典连续系统之间建立了桥梁，展示了离散Hahn代数对称结构如何在连续极限下过渡为Laguerre–Heun代数，为理解超可积系统在不同离散化层次上的代数结构演变提供了统一视角。

Abstract: We construct a discrete Smorodinsky--Winternitz II superintegrable system on a triangular region of the two-dimensional square lattice. The model is built from a pair of commuting finite-difference number operators with finite spectrum together with an associated ladder-operator structure. We show that it is maximally superintegrable and that its symmetry algebra admits a Hahn-algebra presentation. The spectral problem is solved exactly in terms of bivariate orthogonal polynomials of mixed Krawtchouk and dual Hahn type associated with the factorized $A_2$-Leonard pair. Finally, we show that the continuum limit recovers the continuous Smorodinsky--Winternitz II system together with its Hermite--Laguerre eigenfunctions. We further explain how the Hahn presentation of the discrete symmetry algebra becomes singular in this limit, while the limiting algebraic structure is naturally described by the Laguerre--Heun algebra associated with Cartesian and parabolic separation of variables.

</details>


### [150] [Hidden Dyson Universality in Inverse-Spectral Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13475)
*Momo Hayashi,Kazumitsu Sakai*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过dressing变换将展开谱映射为形变谐振子，在振荡子基底下重构算子矩阵元，并利用距离壳矩（distance-shell moments）诊断能级统计的对称性类。结果表明，Riemann zeta函数的非平凡零点重构算子被识别为GUE类，GUE特征不来自直接能级统计，而是从重构算子的矩阵几何中恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dyson universality通常体现在局部本征值统计中，但作者想证明其签名能在非线性逆谱重构过程中存活，并出现在重构算子的矩阵几何中。这提供了研究能级统计普适性的新视角，尤其用于识别如Riemann zeta零点这类非标准矩阵系统的对称性类。

Method: 利用dressing transformation将每个展开谱映射为固定谐振子的形变f(x)，并在共同振荡子基底下表示为F_mn=<m|f|n>。将矩阵元权重按固定距离d=|m-n|（对应参考振荡子的能量转移通道）划分为壳层，定义距离壳矩（distance-shell moments）刻画其分布。该方法先在Gaussian β-系综（GOE/GUE/GSE）上独立标定，这些矩随β平滑变化以区分三类；然后用同一诊断应用到Riemann zeta函数的非平凡零点。

Result: 距离壳矩在Gaussian β-系综中标定后能随β平滑变化并区分GOE、GUE和GSE。将该诊断应用于Riemann zeta函数非平凡零点时，重构算子被置于GUE扇区。

Conclusion: GUE特征不是通过输入能级的直接统计获得的，而是从重构算子的距离分辨几何（distance-resolved geometry）中恢复。Dyson universality的签名在非线性逆谱重构后依然保留，并在重构算子的矩阵几何中重新显现。

Abstract: Dyson universality typically manifests itself in local eigenvalue statistics. Here we show that its signature survives a nonlinear inverse-spectral reconstruction and reappears in the matrix geometry of the reconstructed operator. Using a dressing transformation, we map each unfolded spectrum to a deformation $f(x)$ of a fixed harmonic oscillator and represent it in the common oscillator basis by $F_{mn}=\bra m|f|n\ket$. We resolve the matrix-element weight into shells of fixed distance $d=|m-n|$, corresponding to the energy-transfer channels of the reference oscillator, and characterize the resulting distribution by distance-shell moments. Independently calibrated on Gaussian $β$-ensembles, these moments vary smoothly with $β$ and distinguish the GOE, GUE, and GSE. With this calibration fixed, applying the same diagnostic to the nontrivial zeros of the Riemann zeta function places the reconstructed operators in the GUE sector. Thus, the GUE character of the zeros is recovered not through direct statistics of the input levels, but from the distance-resolved geometry of the reconstructed operator.

</details>


### [151] [Transparent Boundary Conditions for the Heat Equation on Metric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12937)
*Jasur Matrasulov,Jambul Yusupov,Matthias Ehrhardt*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了分支准一维域（度量星图）中时间依赖热方程的透明边界条件（TBCs），通过将经典TBCs与网络PDE理论相结合，推导出确保热流在结点处无阻碍传播的精确顶点条件，即扩散系数的求和规则，并利用Crank-Nicolson有限差分法数值验证了该模型的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分支网络结构（如度量星图）中，热扩散的控制和优化是一个重要的实际问题。传统TBCs在单域中已有成熟理论，但在网络交叉点处的精确条件尚不明确，需要建立严格的数学框架来确保热流穿越结点时无反射或无阻碍传播，从而为低维结构中的热扩散提供可控工具。

Method: 本文的理论方法是将经典透明边界条件（TBCs）理论拓展到度量星图上的偏微分方程框架。具体而言，通过分析结点处的热通量平衡，推导出一个扩散系数的求和规则作为精确的顶点条件，该条件能够消除结点处的热回流（backflow），确保热流在分支结点处无障碍地传播。数值验证采用Crank-Nicolson有限差分格式对网络上的热方程进行离散求解。

Result: 数值实验证明了所推导的顶点求和规则的有效性，Crank-Nicolson有限差分方法的模拟结果确认了热流在网络中平滑、无障碍地传播，验证了解析模型的正确性和可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究成果提供了一个实用的数学框架，可实现对低维结构中热扩散的可调控制和优化。通过将经典TBCs与度量图上的PDE理论相结合，所提出的模型为网络中热扩散提供了有效的控制工具，具有潜在的应用价值。

Abstract: We study transparent boundary conditions (TBCs) for the time-dependent heat equation in a branching, quasi-one-dimensional domain modeled as a metric star graph. By combining the classical concept of TBCs with the theory of partial differential equations (PDEs) on networks, we derive an exact vertex condition that ensures unobstructed thermal flow across junctions. Specifically,we derive a sum rule for the diffusion coefficients that eliminates thermal backflow at the vertex.We demonstrate the validity of this analytical model numerically using a Crank-Nicolson finite-difference method, which confirms smooth, unobstructed heat propagation through the network.These results provide a practical mathematical framework for tunable control and optimization of thermal diffusion in low-dimensional structures. In particular, combining the well-known concept of the TBCs and theory for PDE on metric graphs, we propose a mathematical model providing a control tool for thermal diffusion in networks.

</details>


### [152] [Symmetry Emergence in Self-Organized Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13500)
*Ernesto Lupercio,Mikhail Shkolnikov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了自组织临界性原型模型中最大密度区域内的仿射对称性涌现机制，证明了当格点网格倒数的平方远大于随机扰动点数时，倾倒函数（odometer）的标度极限是带Dirichlet边界条件的Monge-Ampère方程的唯一凹解，从而可以精确估计任意宏观窗口内密度对最大值的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自组织临界性（SOC）是理解复杂系统中临界行为自发涌现的核心框架，而阿贝尔沙堆模型作为其原型模型具有重要的理论地位。理解最大密度区域内倾倒函数的结构及其标度极限，对于揭示SOC系统的普适性机制和密度涨落的统计性质至关重要。

Method: 本文采用解析与归纳论证相结合的方法。核心方法是：在最大密度区域（即网格倒数的平方远大于随机扰动点数）的设定下，建立倾倒函数标度极限与Monge-Ampère方程的联系。具体地，论证该标度极限是带Dirichlet边界条件、以给定概率测度为势的Monge-Ampère方程的唯一凹解，这一结果由归纳论证升级为严格的数学证明。

Result: 证明了在最大密度区域内，倾倒函数的标度极限满足以给定概率测度为势的Monge-Ampère方程，且该解是唯一的。基于此解，可以准确估计任意宏观窗口内密度对最大值的偏差，从而揭示了仿射对称性涌现的机制。

Conclusion: 本文建立了沙堆模型倾倒函数标度极限与最优传输和微分几何中经典Monge-Ampère方程之间的深层联系，为理解自组织临界性系统的普适行为提供了新的解析视角，并给出了密度偏差的精确估计方法。

Abstract: We describe a mechanism of affine symmetry emergence in the maximal density regime of the prototypical model of self-organized criticality when the inverse square of the mesh of the underlying lattice is much larger than the number of random perturbation points distributed according to a prescribed probability measure supported in the interior of the ambient convex domain. Moreover, an appropriate scaling limit of the toppling function (aka odometer), which counts the number of operations per site, is a solution to a non-linear partial differential equation well known in the context of optimal transport and differential geometry, making it possible to accurately estimate the deviation of the density from its maximal value in any macroscopic window. The mechanism for the affine symmetry emergence is due to the novel empirical fact, supported in addition by inductive arguments that have recently being upgraded to a rigorous proof, that the scaling limit of the toppling function is the unique concave solution of the Monge-Ampère equation with Dirichlet boundary condition on the convex domain with the potential given by the probability measure used above as the infinite-perturbation profile.

</details>


### [153] [Exact analytical solution for the non-selfadjoint problem of Maxwell--Cattaneo--Vernotte heat conduction with heat-transfer boundary condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13164)
*Mátyás Szücs,Tamás Fülöp*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了带有传热边界条件的Maxwell–Cattaneo–Vernotte (MCV)双曲型热传导方程的解析解。由于该边界条件使微分算子非自伴，作者通过构造合适的标量积建立了伴随特征值问题，利用左右特征函数构成的双正交系统，将任意平方可积初始条件展开，得到无穷级数形式的解析解。该解在多种模型参数（含近傅里叶和强双曲极限）及两种初始条件（平衡态和闪光脉冲）下得到系统验证，并与有限差分数值解吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越傅里叶热传导模型（如MCV方程）在工程和物理中具有重要意义，但带传热边界条件的MCV方程因算子非自伴而难以解析求解，缺乏可靠的解析基准用于验证数值方法并揭示边界传热与弛豫时间对热传导模式转变的影响。

Method: 通过选取合适的内积构造伴随算子，建立左、右特征函数组成的双正交系统；将任意平方可积初始条件在该系统中展开以确定展开系数，从而得到无穷级数形式的解析解。系统分析了特征值的谱结构（实根、虚根、共轭复根及其渐近分布与对无量纲弛豫时间和Biot数的依赖），并推导了闪光脉冲初始条件的解析表达式。

Result: 解析解在近傅里叶和强双曲极限下与有限差分数值解高度吻合；谱截断效应（Gibbs现象）和热波前附近的数值耗散得到清晰刻画；特征函数集的完备性通过数值验证；闪光脉冲初始条件的解析形式成功推导。

Conclusion: 该工作为非自伴双曲热传导提供了解析基准，揭示了边界传热和弛豫时间如何协同调控傅里叶式扩散与热波传播之间的转变，为实验测量和实际应用中研究和利用超越傅里叶热传导提供了理论工具。

Abstract: The most well-known beyond-Fourier heat conduction model, the Maxwell--Cattaneo--Vernotte equation is solved analytically in the presence of heat transfer boundary condition. In contrast to the corresponding Fourier problem, this boundary condition renders the underlying differential operator non-selfadjoint. With a suitable scalar product, the adjoint eigenvalue problem is established. The resulting left and right eigenfunctions constitute a biorthogonal system, allowing the expansion coefficients to be determined from arbitrary square-integrable initial conditions. This enables a convenient infinite-sum analytical solution, which is presented and thoroughly investigated for various values of the model parameters (including near-Fourier and highly hyperbolic regimes) and for two practically important initial conditions (equilibrium and flash pulse initiated). The spectral structure is analyzed in detail, including the occurrence of real, imaginary, and complex-conjugate eigenvalue roots, their asymptotic distribution, and their dependence on the dimensionless relaxation time and Biot number. We find good agreement with corresponding finite-difference numerical solutions. The comparison also illustrates the effects of spectral truncation (i.e., the Gibbs phenomenon) and numerical dissipation near propagating thermal-wave fronts. Completeness of the eigenfunction set is numerically demonstrated. The initial condition induced by the flash pulse is derived analytically. The results provide an analytical benchmark for non-selfadjoint hyperbolic heat conduction, unveil how boundary heat transfer and relaxation time influence the transition between Fourier-like diffusion and thermal-wave propagation, and open the possibility to find and investigate beyond-Fourier heat conduction via heat transfer in experiments and practical applications.

</details>


### [154] [On Toeplitz determinants with slow Fourier decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13182)
*Nedialko Bradinoff,Maurice Duits*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Fourier系数满足f_k=O(|k|^{-1})的符号f所对应的Toeplitz行列式det T_n(e^f)，该正则性区间超越了H^{1/2}并涵盖Fisher-Hartwig奇异性。作者基于Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff公式发展了一套算子论方法，将log det T_n(e^{tf})展开中的二次项与高阶项分离，证明二次项负责可能随n的增长，而每个固定阶数的高阶系数保持有界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Toeplitz行列式渐近理论通常要求符号在H^{1/2}空间或具有更强的正则性，但对Fourier系数仅满足f_k=O(|k|^{-1})的符号（包括具有Fisher-Hartwig奇异性的符号），现有的结果不够完整。作者希望建立一套统一的框架，能够处理此类更宽泛的符号类，并同时获得行列式的界以及关联的CUE线性统计量的极限定理。

Method: 基于Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff公式的算子论方法。将log det T_n(e^{tf})的展开中的二次项Σ_{k=1}^n min(k,n)f_k f_{-k}与更高阶项分离，分别分析其行为。对于正负Fourier部分均有界的符号，利用估计得到去除二次贡献后行列式的双侧界；对于更广的可容许符号类（包括Fisher-Hartwig型），证明高阶系数的均匀界并导出中心极限定理。

Result: 证明了二次项完全决定行列式可能的增长行为；每个固定阶数的高阶系数保持有界。对满足正负Fourier部分有界的符号，得到了除去二次贡献后的行列式双侧界；对更广的包含Fisher-Hartwig型符号的可容许类，获得了高阶系数的均匀界，以及CUE线性统计量的中心极限定理，并进一步得到超越特征多项式之外的CUE随机场混合指数矩的界。

Conclusion: 该工作将Toeplitz行列式的渐近分析推进到H^{1/2}以外的更弱正则性区域，为处理带奇异性的符号提供了统一且系统的算子论框架。二次项的精确分离与高阶项的有界性刻画为理解行列式增长机制和CUE线性统计量的渐近行为提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We study Toeplitz determinants $\det T_n(e^f)$ for $f$ whose Fourier coefficients satisfy $f_k=O(|k|^{-1})$. This regime extends beyond $H^{1/2}$ and includes symbols with Fisher-Hartwig singularities. We develop an operator-theoretic approach based on the Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff formula that separates the quadratic term \[ \sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\min(k,n)f_kf_{-k} \] from the higher-order terms in the expansion of $\log\det T_n(e^{tf})$. We show that this quadratic term accounts for the possible growth with $n$, while every fixed higher-order coefficient remains bounded. For symbols with bounded positive and negative Fourier parts, our estimates yield two-sided bounds for the determinant after removal of the quadratic contribution. For a broader admissible class, including Fisher-Hartwig-type symbols, we obtain uniform higher-order coefficient bounds and a central limit theorem for the associated CUE linear statistics. We also obtain bounds on mixed exponential moments for CUE-derived random fields beyond the characteristic polynomial.

</details>


### [155] [Proper condensates and the Onsager-Penrose condition: A friendly debate on Bose-Einstein condensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13402)
*Detlev Buchholz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文以辩论形式对比了proper condensate概念与Onsager-Penrose判据在玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚中的适用性，并在尾声讨论了底层预解代数（resolvent algebra）的相关物理性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚存在多种定义和判据，其中proper condensate与Onsager-Penrose判据是两种不同的理论框架，需要澄清它们的区别和各自适用条件。

Method: 采用两位分别支持不同概念的学者之间辩论的文体形式，以序言引入两个判据，以尾声讨论预解代数的物理相关性质。

Result: 以辩论形式呈现了proper condensate与Onsager-Penrose判据之间的对比分析，并在尾声部分对底层预解代数的物理性质给出了评论。

Conclusion: 通过辩论形式对比了两种凝聚判据的优势与局限，并在预解代数的框架下给出了物理上相关的补充性见解。

Abstract: This article contrasts the concepts of a proper condensate and the Onsager-Penrose criterion for Bose-Einstein condensation in the form of a debate between two proponents of the respective concepts. The prologue briefly introduces the two criteria. The epilogue contains remarks on properties of the underlying resolvent algebra that are physically relevant in this context.

</details>
